# PedalMate Phase A (Overlay v0.1-alpha) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Deliver PedalMate Phase A: a sideloaded Android app for the Peloton Bike+ that reads live power/cadence/resistance and Apple-Watch (BLE relay) heart rate, runs Power Zone interval workouts with audio cues, and shows a draggable Compose overlay (plus interval toast) on top of the stock Lichess app. This covers PedalMate.md DoD items 1-5.

**Architecture:** Single `:app` Gradle module, package root `dev.pedalmate`, package-per-subsystem (`sensor`, `heartrate`, `workout`, `ride`, `audio`, `overlay`, `data`, `ui`). `mock`/`real` product flavors differ only by `src/{mock,real}/java/dev/pedalmate/sensor/SensorFactory.kt`. Pure-Kotlin engine code (zones, workout engine, cue policy, connector state machines) is JVM-unit-tested with injected clocks; Android glue (service, WindowManager overlay, BLE) is verified on the emulator and then on the bike.

**Tech Stack:** Kotlin 2.0.21, Jetpack Compose (BOM 2024.09.03), AGP 8.5.2, Gradle 8.9 on JDK 17 (via mise), Room 2.6.1 + KSP, DataStore 1.1.1, kotlinx-coroutines 1.9.0, kotlinx-serialization-json 1.7.3, JUnit4 + Robolectric 4.13 + Turbine 1.1.0.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-04-pedalmate-design.md` (authoritative; decisions are locked). Product doc: `PedalMate.md` (layouts, workout example, DoD). Implementers read the spec sections named in their task, but every rule they need is repeated in this plan.

**Scope:** Tasks A0-A16 only (epics E0 Foundation = A0, A1; EA Phase A = A2-A16 + A3V). Phase B (native chess) is NOT planned here and must not be started.

## Global Constraints

Every task's requirements implicitly include all of these (values copied from the spec):

- Package root `dev.pedalmate`; `namespace = "dev.pedalmate"`; `applicationId = "dev.pedalmate"`, mock flavor `applicationIdSuffix ".mock"` (so the mock APK is `dev.pedalmate.mock`). No "Peloton" or "Lichess" in app name/package/repo name.
- Device is fixed: Peloton Bike+ (board `topaz`, Android 10 / API 29, 1920x1080 landscape). `compileSdk = 34`, **`minSdk = targetSdk = 29`**. `buildFeatures { compose; aidl; buildConfig }`.
- Toolchain: AGP 8.5.2, Kotlin 2.0.21 (+compose, +serialization plugins), KSP 2.0.21-1.0.28, Compose BOM 2024.09.03, activity-compose 1.9.2, lifecycle 2.8.6, Room 2.6.1, coroutines 1.9.0, kotlinx-serialization-json 1.7.3, OkHttp 4.12.0 (+mockwebserver), chesslib 1.3.4 (JitPack `com.github.bhlangonijr:chesslib`, NOT on Maven Central), DataStore 1.1.1, androidx.browser 1.8.0, security-crypto 1.1.0-alpha06, junit 4.13.2, turbine 1.1.0, robolectric 4.13, androidx.test core 1.6.1, androidx.test.ext junit 1.2.1, kotlinx-coroutines-test 1.9.0. Gradle 8.9, JVM 17.
- `testOptions.unitTests { isIncludeAndroidResources = true; isReturnDefaultValues = true }`; `app/src/test/resources/robolectric.properties` contains `application=android.app.Application`. `lint { disable += "ExpiredTargetSdkVersion" }`.
- Gradle is always run as `mise exec -- ./gradlew ...` (default JDK is 26, which Gradle 8.9 cannot run on).
- `settings.gradle.kts` repositories: `google()`, `mavenCentral()`, `maven("https://jitpack.io") { content { includeGroup("com.github.bhlangonijr") } }`.
- Manifest permissions: `SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW`, `FOREGROUND_SERVICE`, `BLUETOOTH` and `BLUETOOTH_ADMIN` (both `maxSdkVersion="30"`), `ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION`, `INTERNET`, `WAKE_LOCK`. `SetupActivity` is the launcher. `RideService` has `android:foregroundServiceType="location|connectedDevice"` and is started with `startForeground(id, notification, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_LOCATION or ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_CONNECTED_DEVICE)` (API 29 introduced the `location` type, needed for BLE scans while another app is in front).
- AIDL package stays `com.onepeloton.affernetservice` (binder descriptor and `readParcelable` class name depend on it). AIDL method order is load-bearing: copy verbatim including placeholder methods.
- Bike+ protocol facts: bind action `com.onepeloton.affernetservice.IBikeInterface`, package `com.onepeloton.affernetservice`; `registerCallback` = transaction 15, `getBikeData` poll = transaction 14; `BikeData`: `rpm` long (rpm), `power` long (centi-watts, divide by 100), `currentResistance` int 0-100; ~21 Hz push. `IV1Interface` is NOT used (it binds but never pushes on Bike+).
- ZoneTable: integer percents 55/75/90/105/120/150; `bound_i = (pct_i * ftp + 99) / 100` (integer ceil); zone i covers `[bound_{i-1}, bound_i - 1]`, Z1 low 0, Z7 `>= bound_6`. FTP = round(0.95 x 20-min avg).
- Overlay window: `TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY`, `FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE`, `PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT`, `WRAP_CONTENT`, gravity `TOP|START`. No Dialog/Popup/DropdownMenu in overlay content.
- Logging tag `PedalMate`; copied OpenRide files keep their own tags: `PelotonBikeIface` (bike source) and `BleHeartRate` (HR source and scanner; see A9 for the one-line tag change that makes the spec's logcat filter work).
- Fair play: no chess-engine, evaluation, or move-hint code anywhere (see "Rules for every task").

## Review Focus

Failure modes the spec implies but a straightforward implementation would miss. Each line has a test pinned in the owning task (named in parentheses):

1. **Identical consecutive frames.** A rider coasting sends many equal `BikeMetrics`; `StateFlow` drops equal values, so liveness/"no frame for 3 s" must be detected from a frame *counter*, not flow emissions. Expected: stays `Connected` while identical frames keep arriving. Same for HR: a steady 72 bpm must not be mistaken for a dead strap. (A2 `BikeSourceSupervisorTest`, A9 `HeartRateConnectorTest`)
2. **Sensor dropout must never look live.** While the bike is not `Connected`, the snapshot has null power, smoothed power, zone, cadence, resistance and target status; the overlay shows "no sensor" and dashes, never the last watts. (A10 `RideSessionTest`, A12 `OverlayUiModelTest`)
3. **FTP not set.** Workouts and free rides must not crash with `ftp == null`: zone/range/status are null, UI shows dashes; zone table never divides by zero; FTP outside 50..600 is rejected in the UI. (A4 `ZoneTableTest`, A6 `WorkoutEngineTest`, A10 `RideSessionTest`, A12 `OverlayUiModelTest`, A14 `FtpInputTest` and `SetupViewModelTest`)
4. **Process death or service restart mid-ride.** An unfinished ride row (no `endedAt`) is finalised from its last sample on next app start; a `START_STICKY` restart with a null intent and no live session stops itself instead of showing a phantom ride. (A8 `RideFinalizerTest`, A10 `RideServicePolicyTest`)
5. **Stalled ticker / huge or negative time deltas** (screen off, doze, clock hiccup): the engine must emit every `IntervalChanged` in order, never negative remaining time, ignore negative deltas, and never emit a burst of stale countdown beeps. (A6 `WorkoutEngineTest`, A10 `RideSessionTest`)

Also covered by explicit tests: BLE scan failure (location services off) backs off and retries without crashing (A9); overlay permission missing never crashes the service (A11, A15); `ftp-test` preset has a null-zone step (A5, A6, A7 `CuePolicy` treats null zone as neutral).

## Rules for every task

These apply to every task below. Implementers get this section plus their own task.

1. **Roles.** Sonnet implements one task per fresh session; Opus reviews the diff plus test output; the main session only orchestrates. Do not re-open locked decisions. If the task is blocked or the plan contradicts reality (a file/API is missing), stop and report instead of improvising a different design.
2. **TDD.** Write the failing test first, run it and see it fail for the right reason, then implement, then see it pass. Pure-Kotlin logic gets JVM unit tests (no Robolectric unless the code needs `Looper`/`Parcel`/`Context`). Tests use injected clocks / fake collaborators, never real time or `Thread.sleep`.
3. **Verify command** (after A1 exists): `mise exec -- ./gradlew testMockDebugUnitTest assembleMockDebug assembleRealDebug`. Run it before every commit; it must end with `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`. Tasks that precede the scaffold use the narrower command stated in the task. When a task says "run the new test", use `mise exec -- ./gradlew testMockDebugUnitTest --tests 'dev.pedalmate.<pkg>.<Class>'`.
4. **Commits.** One commit per task, using the message given in the task (subject line verbatim; add the attribution trailer your harness requires, preceded by a blank line). Stage only the files the task lists, by explicit path (`git add path1 path2`), never `git add -A` / `git add .`. Never stage `.beads/`. Do not push.
5. **Fair play (hard rule).** No code anywhere that analyses a chess position, evaluates moves, suggests moves, ships an engine (Stockfish etc.), or opening book. Phase A has no chess code at all; the overlay shows cycling data only. A reviewer checks `git diff` for violations.
6. **OpenRide copy rule (Apache-2.0 section 4).**
   - Every copied or adapted file's first line is exactly:
     `// Derived from OpenRide (Apache-2.0), <original path>. Modified by PedalMate.`
     where `<original path>` is the repo-relative OpenRide path from the table in the task (for example `app/src/main/aidl/com/onepeloton/affernetservice/IBikeInterface.aidl`). This applies to Kotlin, AIDL and test files. OpenRide files have no header of their own to preserve; keep their other comments.
   - `NOTICE` reproduces OpenRide's NOTICE verbatim (created in A1; never edit the verbatim block).
   - Grupetto (a different project, no licence) is reference-only: copy nothing from it.
   - Reviewer check: `grep -rL "Derived from OpenRide" <list of copied files>` prints nothing.
7. **Fetching OpenRide source.** Repo `digitalducktape/OpenRide` (Apache-2.0; found with `gh search repos OpenRide peloton`), branch `main`, pinned to commit `6d88cc005b53d95505d6a5c85c37757a117d0a74`. Do NOT search again. **Trap:** the local `gh` wrapper prints a stray first line (`mise ~/.config/mise/config.toml tools: gh@2.97.0`) to **stdout**, which corrupts fetched files. Always use this helper, which strips it:
   ```bash
   orfetch() { gh api "repos/digitalducktape/OpenRide/contents/$1?ref=6d88cc005b53d95505d6a5c85c37757a117d0a74" -H "Accept: application/vnd.github.raw" 2>/dev/null | sed '1{/^mise /d}'; }
   # example: orfetch app/src/main/aidl/com/onepeloton/affernetservice/IBikeInterface.aidl > /tmp/x.aidl
   ```
   After fetching, `head -3` the result to confirm no `mise ...` line remains.
8. **Logging** uses `android.util.Log` with tag `PedalMate` in new code. No `println`, no log spam inside the 21 Hz frame path (periodic logs only).
9. **Style.** Kotlin official style, 4-space indent, explicit visibility only where it matters. KDoc on public types. No emoji anywhere (code, docs, commit messages). Unicode in user-visible strings uses escapes where noted (en dash `\u2013`, greater-or-equal `\u2265`).
10. **Do not edit** `docs/superpowers/specs/...` except where a task explicitly says so (A5 appends the workout JSON schema section).

## Shared interface cheat sheet

Names used across tasks (each task's "Interfaces" block repeats what it needs):

```kotlin
// dev.pedalmate.sensor
data class BikeMetrics(val cadenceRpm: Int, val resistancePercent: Int, val powerWatts: Int) { companion object { val ZERO } }
sealed interface ConnectionState { data object Connected; data object Disconnected; data object Unavailable }
interface BikeDataSource { val metrics: StateFlow<BikeMetrics>; val connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState> }
interface BoundBikeDataSource : BikeDataSource { fun start(); fun stop() }
interface PollableBikeDataSource : BoundBikeDataSource { val framesReceived: Long; fun pollBikeData(): BikeMetrics? }
// SensorFactory (per flavor): createBikeSource(context, scope): BoundBikeDataSource;
//   createHeartRateSource(context, scope, store: SavedHrDeviceStore): ManagedHeartRateDataSource; createBleScanner(context): BleScanner
// dev.pedalmate.heartrate
interface HeartRateDataSource { val bpm: StateFlow<Int?>; val connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState> }
interface ManagedHeartRateDataSource : HeartRateDataSource { fun start(); fun stop() }
interface HeartRateLink : ManagedHeartRateDataSource { val framesReceived: Long }
data class SavedHrDevice(val address: String, val name: String?)
interface SavedHrDeviceStore { fun load(): SavedHrDevice?; fun save(device: SavedHrDevice?) }
class HeartRateConnector(scanner: BleScanner, linkFactory: (String) -> HeartRateLink, store: SavedHrDeviceStore, clock: () -> Long, scheduler: TickScheduler) : ManagedHeartRateDataSource { fun useDevice(device: BleDevice); fun forget() }
class HrPairing(hr, store) { fun use(device: BleDevice); fun forget() }; class HrPairingController(scanner, pairing, scope)   // A14H
// dev.pedalmate.workout
enum class PowerZone(number: Int, upperPercent: Int?)   // no label property
data class ZoneRange(zone: PowerZone, lowWatts: Int, highWatts: Int?) { val isEmpty }; class ZoneTable { companion object { fun forFtp(ftp: Int?): ZoneTable? } }
enum class TargetStatus { BELOW, IN, ABOVE }; class TargetStatusTracker; class PowerSmoother; object FtpCalculator
// WorkoutDefinition/WorkoutStep, WorkoutRepository(assets).load(): LoadResult(workouts, errors: Map<String,String>), WorkoutEngine(definition, zoneTable?), WorkoutState, WorkoutEvent, StepInfo
// dev.pedalmate.audio: enum Cue; object CuePolicy; fun interface CueSink { fun play(cue: Cue) }; class CuePlayer : CueSink
// dev.pedalmate.data: AppDatabase, SettingsStore, Settings, SettingsHrDeviceStore, AppContainer, ContextAssetReader
// dev.pedalmate.ride: RideFrame, RideLog, LiveAggregates, RideRecorder : RideLog, RideFinalizer,
//   RideStatus, RideSnapshot, SensorHub, StartResult, RideSession, RideServicePolicy, RideService
// dev.pedalmate.overlay: OverlayPosition, OverlayGeometry, OverlayController, OverlayWindow, ComposeOverlayHost,
//   formatClock/formatRange, OverlayUiModel, OverlayContent, OverlayPolicy, OverlayPrefs, ToastModel, IntervalToast, OverlayToastWindow
// dev.pedalmate.permissions: PermissionPlanner, PermissionHelper
// dev.pedalmate.ui.setup: FtpInput, LichessLauncher, RideCommands, SetupViewModel, SetupActivity
```

### Task A0: Dev env + docs

**Goal:** A working build environment: JDK 17 pinned via mise, Gradle 8.9 wrapper committed, Android SDK pieces installed, AVD `pm29` (1920x1080 @160dpi landscape, Android 10) created, and a `.gitignore` for build output.

**Files:**
- Create: `mise.toml`, `gradlew`, `gradlew.bat`, `gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar`, `gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties`
- Modify: `.gitignore` (APPEND; it already contains beads/Dolt lines that must stay)
- Not committed: SDK packages, AVD (live in `~/Android` and `~/.android`)
- Already committed (verify only): `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-04-pedalmate-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-04-phase-a.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `mise exec -- java -version` = 17; `mise exec -- ./gradlew --version` works; AVD named `pm29`; `ANDROID_HOME` set through mise.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create `mise.toml`**

```toml
[tools]
java = "zulu-17"

[env]
ANDROID_HOME = "{{env.HOME}}/Android/Sdk"
```

Then run `mise trust` once in the repo root.

- [ ] **Step 2: Verify the JDK pin**

Run: `mise exec -- java -version 2>&1 | head -1`
Expected: a line containing `17.` (zulu-17.46.19.0 is already installed; no download should happen). If mise tries to download a different JDK, check `mise ls java` and use the exact installed `zulu-17.x` name in `mise.toml`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Append build-output ignores to `.gitignore`**

Append (do not overwrite; keep existing `.dolt/` and `*.db` lines):

```gitignore

# Gradle / Android
.gradle/
build/
local.properties
*.iml
.idea/
.DS_Store
captures/
.externalNativeBuild/
.cxx/
*.apk
*.aab
*.keystore
```

Confirm `docs/` is NOT ignored: `git check-ignore -v docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-04-phase-a.md` must print nothing. Do not ignore `*.jar` (the wrapper jar is committed) or `app/schemas/`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Install SDK packages**

```bash
yes | sdkmanager --licenses >/dev/null
sdkmanager "platforms;android-34" "build-tools;34.0.0" "system-images;android-29;google_apis;x86_64"
```
(`sdkmanager` is `~/Android/Sdk/cmdline-tools/latest/bin/sdkmanager`, already on PATH.) Expected: all three install. Platforms 30/33/36 and build-tools 33/36 already exist.

- [ ] **Step 5: Bootstrap the Gradle wrapper (Gradle 8.9 cannot run on JDK 26, so use mise)**

```bash
mise exec java@17 gradle@8.9 -- gradle wrapper --gradle-version 8.9
```
If mise reports it would install a new JDK for `java@17`, use the installed name instead: `mise exec java@zulu-17.46.19.0 gradle@8.9 -- gradle wrapper --gradle-version 8.9`.
Verify: `mise exec -- ./gradlew --version | head -5` shows Gradle 8.9 and a JVM 17 line. (No settings file exists yet, which is fine for `--version`.)

- [ ] **Step 6: Create the AVD**

```bash
echo no | avdmanager create avd -n pm29 -k "system-images;android-29;google_apis;x86_64" -d pixel_c
```
Edit `~/.android/avd/pm29.avd/config.ini`: set (replace existing lines or append) `hw.lcd.width=1920`, `hw.lcd.height=1080`, `hw.lcd.density=160`, `hw.initialOrientation=landscape`, `hw.keyboard=yes`. Remove any `skin.name=` / `skin.path=` lines (the pixel_c skin would override the resolution) and add `skin.dynamic=yes`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Boot-check the AVD (headless is fine)**

```bash
emulator -avd pm29 -no-window -no-audio -no-snapshot -gpu swiftshader_indirect >/dev/null 2>&1 &
adb wait-for-device
until [ "$(adb shell getprop sys.boot_completed | tr -d '\r')" = "1" ]; do sleep 5; done
adb shell wm size; adb shell wm density; adb shell getprop ro.build.version.sdk
```
Expected: `Physical size: 1920x1080`, `Physical density: 160`, sdk `29`. Then `adb emu kill`. If the emulator cannot start in this environment (no KVM/display), report that clearly rather than hacking around it; the AVD existing (`avdmanager list avd | grep pm29`) plus a documented boot failure is acceptable for A0.

- [ ] **Step 8: Commit**

```bash
git add mise.toml .gitignore gradlew gradlew.bat gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties
git commit -m "chore: dev environment (mise JDK 17, Gradle 8.9 wrapper, gitignore)"
```

**Acceptance:** `mise exec -- java -version` = 17; `./gradlew --version` = Gradle 8.9 on JVM 17; AVD `pm29` exists (resolution check from step 7 if the emulator can run); spec and plan docs are tracked in git; `git status` shows no untracked build junk.

### Task A1: Gradle scaffold

**Goal:** A buildable Compose app with `mock`/`real` flavors, licence files, and a stub `SetupActivity`, so every later task has a green verify command.

**Files:**
- Create: `settings.gradle.kts`, `build.gradle.kts`, `gradle.properties`, `gradle/libs.versions.toml`
- Create: `app/build.gradle.kts`, `app/proguard-rules.pro`, `app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`

### Task A2: Sensor import (real Bike+ source)

**Goal:** Import OpenRide's Affernet AIDL, `BikeData` parcels and `IBikeInterface` data source, adapt them to `dev.pedalmate.sensor`, and add `BikeSourceSupervisor` (push-silence poll fallback and a 3 s dead-link detector driven by a frame counter, with injected clock and scheduler). Add the `real` flavor `SensorFactory.createBikeSource`.

- Produces (all in `dev.pedalmate.sensor`):
```kotlin
data class BikeMetrics(val cadenceRpm: Int, val resistancePercent: Int, val powerWatts: Int) { companion object { val ZERO = BikeMetrics(0, 0, 0) } }
sealed interface ConnectionState { data object Connected; data object Disconnected; data object Unavailable }
interface BikeDataSource { val metrics: StateFlow<BikeMetrics>; val connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState> }
interface BoundBikeDataSource : BikeDataSource { fun start(); fun stop() }
interface PollableBikeDataSource : BoundBikeDataSource { val framesReceived: Long; fun pollBikeData(): BikeMetrics? }
interface TickScheduler { fun start(periodMs: Long, tick: () -> Unit); fun stop() }
class CoroutineTickScheduler(scope: CoroutineScope) : TickScheduler
class BikeSourceSupervisor(source: PollableBikeDataSource, clock: () -> Long, scheduler: TickScheduler) : BoundBikeDataSource
// real flavor only (this task): SensorFactory.createBikeSource(context: Context, scope: CoroutineScope): BoundBikeDataSource
// test helpers (src/test): FakeClock, FakeTickScheduler, TickHarness in package dev.pedalmate.testutil (reused by A9)
```

```bash
HDR() { echo "// Derived from OpenRide (Apache-2.0), $1. Modified by PedalMate."; }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests (mapping, supervisor, harness)**

`app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/testutil/TickHarness.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.testutil

import dev.pedalmate.sensor.TickScheduler

/** Settable monotonic clock for deterministic tests. */
class FakeClock(var nowMs: Long = 0L) : () -> Long {
    override fun invoke(): Long = nowMs
}

/** Scheduler whose ticks are fired by the test, never by real time. */
class FakeTickScheduler : TickScheduler {
    var running = false
        private set
    var periodMs = 0L
        private set
    private var tick: (() -> Unit)? = null

    override fun start(periodMs: Long, tick: () -> Unit) {
        this.periodMs = periodMs
        this.tick = tick
        running = true
    }

    override fun stop() {
        running = false
        tick = null
    }

    fun fire() {
        if (running) tick?.invoke()
    }
}

/** Advances [clock] in [stepMs] steps, firing one scheduler tick after each step. */
class TickHarness(val clock: FakeClock, val scheduler: FakeTickScheduler) {
    fun advanceBy(ms: Long, stepMs: Long = 250L) {
        var left = ms
        while (left > 0) {
            val step = minOf(stepMs, left)
            clock.nowMs += step
            scheduler.fire()
            left -= step
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/sensor/BikeDataMappingTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.sensor

import com.onepeloton.affernetservice.BikeData
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class BikeDataMappingTest {
    private fun frame(rpm: Long = 0, power: Long = 0, res: Int = 0) = BikeData().apply {
        this.rpm = rpm; this.power = power; this.currentResistance = res
    }

    @Test fun `power is centi-watts divided by 100`() {
        assertEquals(183, frame(power = 18_300).toBikeMetrics().powerWatts)
        assertEquals(183, frame(power = 18_399).toBikeMetrics().powerWatts)   // truncates
        assertEquals(0, frame(power = 99).toBikeMetrics().powerWatts)
        assertEquals(0, frame(power = 0).toBikeMetrics().powerWatts)
    }

    @Test fun `cadence maps straight through`() {
        assertEquals(87, frame(rpm = 87).toBikeMetrics().cadenceRpm)
    }

    @Test fun `resistance is clamped to 0..100`() {
        assertEquals(44, frame(res = 44).toBikeMetrics().resistancePercent)
        assertEquals(100, frame(res = 101).toBikeMetrics().resistancePercent)
        assertEquals(0, frame(res = -5).toBikeMetrics().resistancePercent)
    }

    @Test fun `defensive clamps keep garbage frames in range`() {
        assertEquals(0, frame(power = -500).toBikeMetrics().powerWatts)
        assertEquals(9_999, frame(power = 1_000_000).toBikeMetrics().powerWatts)
        assertEquals(0, frame(rpm = -3).toBikeMetrics().cadenceRpm)
        assertEquals(999, frame(rpm = 5_000).toBikeMetrics().cadenceRpm)
    }
}
```
(`toBikeMetrics` is `internal`; same module test source sees it. If constructing `BikeData()` fails on the plain JVM because of Android stubs, add `@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)` like `BikeDataParcelTest`.)

`app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/sensor/BikeSourceSupervisorTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.sensor

import dev.pedalmate.testutil.FakeClock
import dev.pedalmate.testutil.FakeTickScheduler
import dev.pedalmate.testutil.TickHarness
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

private val M = BikeMetrics(cadenceRpm = 87, resistancePercent = 44, powerWatts = 183)

private class FakeSource : PollableBikeDataSource {
    override val metrics = MutableStateFlow(BikeMetrics.ZERO)
    override val connectionState = MutableStateFlow<ConnectionState>(ConnectionState.Unavailable)
    override var framesReceived: Long = 0L
    var pollResult: BikeMetrics? = null
    var pollCount = 0
    var started = 0
    var stopped = 0

    override fun start() { started++ }
    override fun stop() { stopped++ }
    override fun pollBikeData(): BikeMetrics? {
        pollCount++
        val r = pollResult
        if (r != null) { framesReceived++; metrics.value = r }
        return r
    }
    /** Simulates one pushed frame (a StateFlow would drop equal values; the counter must not). */
    fun pushFrame(m: BikeMetrics = M) { framesReceived++; metrics.value = m }
}

class BikeSourceSupervisorTest {
    private val source = FakeSource()
    private val clock = FakeClock()
    private val scheduler = FakeTickScheduler()
    private val harness = TickHarness(clock, scheduler)
    private val supervisor = BikeSourceSupervisor(source, clock, scheduler)
    private val state get() = supervisor.connectionState.value

    @Test fun `start and stop drive source and scheduler`() {
        supervisor.start()
        assertEquals(1, source.started)
        assertTrue(scheduler.running)
        assertEquals(250L, scheduler.periodMs)
        supervisor.stop()
        assertEquals(1, source.stopped)
        assertFalse(scheduler.running)
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Unavailable, state)
    }

    @Test fun `stays Unavailable until the first frame`() {
        supervisor.start()
        harness.advanceBy(1_000)
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Unavailable, state)
        source.pushFrame()
        harness.advanceBy(250)
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Connected, state)
    }

    @Test fun `identical consecutive frames stay Connected and never trigger polling`() {
        supervisor.start()
        repeat(40) { source.pushFrame(M); harness.advanceBy(250) }   // 10 s of the same reading
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Connected, state)
        assertEquals(0, source.pollCount)
    }

    @Test fun `silent source is polled at 1 Hz starting 5 s after start`() {
        supervisor.start()
        harness.advanceBy(4_750)
        assertEquals(0, source.pollCount)
        harness.advanceBy(250)                  // t = 5000
        assertEquals(1, source.pollCount)
        harness.advanceBy(3_000)                // polls at 6000, 7000, 8000
        assertEquals(4, source.pollCount)
    }

    @Test fun `resumed push frames stop polling`() {
        supervisor.start()
        harness.advanceBy(6_000)                // polls at 5000 and 6000
        assertEquals(2, source.pollCount)
        source.pushFrame(); source.pushFrame()
        harness.advanceBy(250)                  // supervisor sees pushes, polling off
        val polls = source.pollCount
        harness.advanceBy(4_000)                // < 5 s since last push
        assertEquals(polls, source.pollCount)
    }

    @Test fun `3 s without any frame reports Disconnected`() {
        supervisor.start()
        source.pushFrame()
        harness.advanceBy(250)                  // t = 250, Connected
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Connected, state)
        harness.advanceBy(2_750)                // t = 3000, gap 2750
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Connected, state)
        harness.advanceBy(250)                  // t = 3250, gap 3000
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Disconnected, state)
    }

    @Test fun `a successful poll restores Connected`() {
        supervisor.start()
        source.pushFrame()
        harness.advanceBy(3_250)                // Disconnected
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Disconnected, state)
        source.pollResult = M
        harness.advanceBy(2_000)                // t = 5250: 5 s since last push, first poll succeeds
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Connected, state)
    }

    @Test fun `a failing poll leaves Disconnected`() {
        supervisor.start()
        source.pushFrame()
        harness.advanceBy(10_000)
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Disconnected, state)
        assertTrue(source.pollCount > 0)
    }

    @Test fun `restart after stop forgets the old session`() {
        supervisor.start()
        source.pushFrame()
        harness.advanceBy(250)
        supervisor.stop()
        supervisor.start()
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Unavailable, state)
        harness.advanceBy(1_000)
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Unavailable, state)
        source.pushFrame()
        harness.advanceBy(250)
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Connected, state)
    }

    @Test fun `metrics pass through from the wrapped source`() {
        source.pushFrame(M)
        assertEquals(M, supervisor.metrics.value)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `TickScheduler.kt` and `BikeSourceSupervisor.kt`**

`TickScheduler.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.sensor

import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

/** Repeats a callback at a fixed period; injected so state machines are testable without real time. */
interface TickScheduler {
    /** Starts calling [tick] every [periodMs] until [stop]. A second start replaces the first. */
    fun start(periodMs: Long, tick: () -> Unit)

    /** Stops ticking. Safe to call when not started. */
    fun stop()
}

/** Production [TickScheduler] backed by a coroutine in [scope]. */
class CoroutineTickScheduler(private val scope: CoroutineScope) : TickScheduler {
    private var job: Job? = null

    @Synchronized
    override fun start(periodMs: Long, tick: () -> Unit) {
        job?.cancel()
        job = scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(periodMs)
                tick()
            }
        }
    }

    @Synchronized
    override fun stop() {
        job?.cancel()
        job = null
    }
}
```

`BikeSourceSupervisor.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.sensor

import android.util.Log
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

/**
 * Wraps the Bike+ source with two safety nets:
 *  - if no frame is PUSHED for [PUSH_SILENCE_MS], poll `getBikeData` at 1 Hz until pushes resume;
 *  - if no frame (pushed or polled) arrives for [DISCONNECT_MS], report [ConnectionState.Disconnected].
 * Liveness comes from [PollableBikeDataSource.framesReceived], never from StateFlow emissions,
 * because identical consecutive frames are legitimate and a StateFlow would swallow them.
 * Time comes from the injected [clock] (monotonic ms) and [scheduler]; no real time in here.
 */
class BikeSourceSupervisor(
    private val source: PollableBikeDataSource,
    private val clock: () -> Long,
    private val scheduler: TickScheduler,
) : BoundBikeDataSource {

    override val metrics: StateFlow<BikeMetrics> get() = source.metrics

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow<ConnectionState>(ConnectionState.Unavailable)
    override val connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private var framesSeen = 0L
    private var lastPushAt = 0L
    private var lastFrameAt = 0L
    private var lastPollAt = 0L
    private var lastLogAt = 0L
    private var everFrame = false
    private var polling = false

    @Synchronized
    override fun start() {
        val now = clock()
        framesSeen = source.framesReceived
        lastPushAt = now; lastFrameAt = now; lastPollAt = now; lastLogAt = now
        everFrame = false
        polling = false
        _state.value = ConnectionState.Unavailable
        source.start()
        scheduler.start(TICK_MS) { onTick() }
    }

    @Synchronized
    override fun stop() {
        scheduler.stop()
        source.stop()
        polling = false
        everFrame = false
        _state.value = ConnectionState.Unavailable
    }

    @Synchronized
    private fun onTick() {
        val now = clock()
        val count = source.framesReceived
        if (count != framesSeen) {                       // frames were pushed since we last looked
            framesSeen = count
            lastPushAt = now; lastFrameAt = now; everFrame = true
            if (polling) { polling = false; Log.i(TAG, "push resumed, polling stopped") }
        }
        if (!polling && now - lastPushAt >= PUSH_SILENCE_MS) {
            polling = true
            lastPollAt = now - POLL_PERIOD_MS            // first poll happens on this tick
            Log.i(TAG, "no push for ${PUSH_SILENCE_MS}ms, polling getBikeData at 1 Hz")
        }
        if (polling && now - lastPollAt >= POLL_PERIOD_MS) {
            lastPollAt = now
            if (source.pollBikeData() != null) { lastFrameAt = now; everFrame = true }
            framesSeen = source.framesReceived           // our own poll is not a push
        }
        _state.value = when {
            everFrame && now - lastFrameAt < DISCONNECT_MS -> ConnectionState.Connected
            everFrame -> ConnectionState.Disconnected
            else -> ConnectionState.Unavailable
        }
        if (now - lastLogAt >= LOG_EVERY_MS) {
            lastLogAt = now
            Log.i(TAG, "bike frames=${source.framesReceived} state=${_state.value} polling=$polling")
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "PedalMate"
        const val TICK_MS = 250L
        const val PUSH_SILENCE_MS = 5_000L
        const val POLL_PERIOD_MS = 1_000L
        const val DISCONNECT_MS = 3_000L
        private const val LOG_EVERY_MS = 5_000L
    }
}
```

Profile definition (tests below pin it): cycle = 7 zone blocks of 20 s (140 s ramp Z1..Z7) then, when `dropouts`, 6 s of dropout (`sample` returns null), cycle length 146 s (140 s without dropouts). Block index `z = (t % cycle) / 20`. Watts = `round(ftp * FRACTION[z]) + noise(-5..5)` with `FRACTION = [0.40, 0.65, 0.82, 0.97, 1.12, 1.35, 1.70]` (for ftp 200 the block centres 80/130/164/194/224/270/340 W sit in zones Z1..Z7 of the A4 table). Cadence `60 + 4*z + noise(-2..2)`; resistance `(20 + 8*z + noise(-2..2)).coerceIn(0,100)`. Noise comes from `kotlin.random.Random(seed * 1_000_003L + tSec)` so a sample depends only on `(seed, t)`; draw watts noise first, then cadence, then resistance.

```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.sensor

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import kotlin.math.abs

class ScriptedProfileTest {
    @Test fun `same seed gives identical samples regardless of call order`() {
        val a = ScriptedProfile(seed = 7)
        val b = ScriptedProfile(seed = 7)
        val forward = (0..300).map { a.sample(it) }
        val backward = (300 downTo 0).map { b.sample(it) }.reversed()
        assertEquals(forward, backward)
    }

    @Test fun `different seeds differ somewhere`() {
        val a = (0..139).map { ScriptedProfile(seed = 1).sample(it) }
        val b = (0..139).map { ScriptedProfile(seed = 2).sample(it) }
        assertNotEquals(a, b)
    }

    @Test fun `each 20 s block sits at its zone fraction of FTP within noise`() {
        val fractions = doubleArrayOf(0.40, 0.65, 0.82, 0.97, 1.12, 1.35, 1.70)
        val p = ScriptedProfile(seed = 3, ftp = 200)
        for (z in 0..6) {
            val m = p.sample(z * 20 + 10)!!
            val centre = Math.round(200 * fractions[z]).toInt()
            assertTrue("zone block $z watts=${m.powerWatts}", abs(m.powerWatts - centre) <= 5)
        }
    }

    @Test fun `six second dropout after the ramp then it resumes`() {
        val p = ScriptedProfile(seed = 1)
        assertNotNull(p.sample(139))
        for (t in 140..145) assertNull("t=$t", p.sample(t))
        assertNotNull(p.sample(146))
        assertNull(p.sample(146 + 140))
    }

    @Test fun `dropouts can be disabled`() {
        val p = ScriptedProfile(seed = 1, dropouts = false)
        assertTrue((0..600).all { p.sample(it) != null })
    }

    @Test fun `cadence and resistance stay in range`() {
        val p = ScriptedProfile(seed = 5)
        (0..600).mapNotNull { p.sample(it) }.forEach {
            assertTrue(it.cadenceRpm in 0..150)
            assertTrue(it.resistancePercent in 0..100)
            assertTrue(it.powerWatts >= 0)
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.sensor

import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.advanceTimeBy
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runCurrent
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class MockBikeDataSourceTest {
    @Test fun `unavailable until started then Connected with the first sample`() = runTest {
        val src = MockBikeDataSource(ScriptedProfile(seed = 1), backgroundScope)
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Unavailable, src.connectionState.value)
        src.start(); runCurrent()
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Connected, src.connectionState.value)
        assertEquals(ScriptedProfile(seed = 1).sample(0), src.metrics.value)
    }

    @Test fun `dropout flips to Disconnected and recovers`() = runTest {
        val src = MockBikeDataSource(ScriptedProfile(seed = 1), backgroundScope)
        src.start(); runCurrent()
        advanceTimeBy(140_000L); runCurrent()          // tick for t = 140 s
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Disconnected, src.connectionState.value)
        advanceTimeBy(6_000L); runCurrent()            // t = 146 s
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Connected, src.connectionState.value)
    }

    @Test fun `stop makes it Unavailable and silent`() = runTest {
        val src = MockBikeDataSource(ScriptedProfile(seed = 1), backgroundScope)
        src.start(); runCurrent()
        src.stop()
        val frozen = src.metrics.value
        advanceTimeBy(30_000L); runCurrent()
        assertEquals(ConnectionState.Unavailable, src.connectionState.value)
        assertEquals(frozen, src.metrics.value)
    }

    @Test fun `start twice does not double the tick rate`() = runTest {
        val src = MockBikeDataSource(ScriptedProfile(seed = 1), backgroundScope, tickMs = 1_000L)
        src.start(); src.start(); runCurrent()
        advanceTimeBy(10_000L); runCurrent()
        assertEquals(ScriptedProfile(seed = 1).sample(10), src.metrics.value)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `ScriptedProfile`, `MockBikeDataSource`, mock `SensorFactory`**

`ScriptedProfile.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.sensor

import kotlin.math.roundToInt
import kotlin.random.Random

/** Deterministic scripted ride for emulator work: Z1..Z7 ramp with noise and a periodic dropout. */
class ScriptedProfile(
    private val seed: Long = 1L,
    private val ftp: Int = 200,
    private val dropouts: Boolean = true,
) {
    private val cycleSec = if (dropouts) CYCLE_WITH_DROPOUT_SEC else RAMP_SEC

    /** Reading for second [tSec] of the script, or null while the sensor is "dropped out". */
    fun sample(tSec: Int): BikeMetrics? {
        require(tSec >= 0) { "tSec must be >= 0" }
        val t = tSec % cycleSec
        if (t >= RAMP_SEC) return null
        val z = t / ZONE_BLOCK_SEC
        val rnd = Random(seed * 1_000_003L + tSec)
        val watts = (ftp * FRACTION[z]).roundToInt() + rnd.nextInt(-5, 6)
        val cadence = 60 + 4 * z + rnd.nextInt(-2, 3)
        val resistance = 20 + 8 * z + rnd.nextInt(-2, 3)
        return BikeMetrics(
            cadenceRpm = cadence.coerceAtLeast(0),
            resistancePercent = resistance.coerceIn(0, 100),
            powerWatts = watts.coerceAtLeast(0),
        )
    }

    private companion object {
        val FRACTION = doubleArrayOf(0.40, 0.65, 0.82, 0.97, 1.12, 1.35, 1.70)
        const val ZONE_BLOCK_SEC = 20
        const val RAMP_SEC = 140
        const val CYCLE_WITH_DROPOUT_SEC = 146
    }
}
```
`MockBikeDataSource.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.sensor

import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

/** Emulator stand-in for the Bike+: replays [profile] at [tickMs]; script time counts ticks, not wall time. */
class MockBikeDataSource(
    private val profile: ScriptedProfile,
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
    private val tickMs: Long = 250L,
) : BoundBikeDataSource {
    private val _metrics = MutableStateFlow(BikeMetrics.ZERO)
    override val metrics: StateFlow<BikeMetrics> = _metrics.asStateFlow()
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow<ConnectionState>(ConnectionState.Unavailable)
    override val connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    private var job: Job? = null

    @Synchronized
    override fun start() {
        if (job?.isActive == true) return
        job = scope.launch {
            var tick = 0L
            while (isActive) {
                val sample = profile.sample((tick * tickMs / 1_000L).toInt())
                if (sample != null) {
                    _metrics.value = sample
                    _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
                } else {
                    _state.value = ConnectionState.Disconnected   // metrics keep the stale value on purpose
                }
                tick++
                delay(tickMs)
            }
        }
    }

    @Synchronized
    override fun stop() {
        job?.cancel()
        job = null
        _state.value = ConnectionState.Unavailable
    }
}
```

```markdown
# Phase A on-bike verification

Device: Peloton Bike+ (board `topaz`, Android 10). Pair ADB first (OpenPelo wireless ADB). Package for real builds: `dev.pedalmate`.

## Stage 1 (after A3): sensor proof
```

### Task A4: Zone maths

**Goal:** Pure-Kotlin power-zone model: `PowerZone`, `ZoneTable` (integer-percent bounds), `FtpCalculator`, `PowerSmoother` (3 s window) and `TargetStatusTracker` (below/in/above with tolerance and debounce). No Android imports.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/workout/PowerZone.kt`, `ZoneTable.kt`, `FtpCalculator.kt`, `PowerSmoother.kt`, `TargetStatus.kt` (same directory)
- Create: `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/workout/ZoneTableTest.kt`, `FtpCalculatorTest.kt`, `PowerSmootherTest.kt`, `TargetStatusTrackerTest.kt`

OpenRide has `PowerZone.kt` / `FtpEstimator.kt` (`app/src/main/java/dev/digitalducktape/openride/core/ride/`), but they use floating-point fractions, which differ from the integer rule below. Write this code fresh. If you do copy any text from them, add the "Derived from OpenRide" header (Rules, item 6).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces (package `dev.pedalmate.workout`):
```kotlin
enum class PowerZone(val number: Int, val upperPercent: Int?) {   // Z1(1,55) Z2(2,75) Z3(3,90) Z4(4,105) Z5(5,120) Z6(6,150) Z7(7,null)
    ; companion object { fun fromNumber(n: Int): PowerZone? }
}
data class ZoneRange(val zone: PowerZone, val lowWatts: Int, val highWatts: Int?) { val isEmpty: Boolean }  // highWatts == null for Z7; isEmpty = high != null && high < low
class ZoneTable private constructor(val ftp: Int) {
    val ranges: List<ZoneRange>                    // Z1..Z7 in order
    fun rangeOf(zone: PowerZone): ZoneRange
    fun zoneFor(watts: Int): PowerZone             // negative watts count as 0
    companion object { fun forFtp(ftp: Int?): ZoneTable? }   // null for null, <= 0 or > 1_000_000
}
object FtpCalculator {
    const val MIN_FTP = 50; const val MAX_FTP = 600
    fun fromTwentyMinuteAverage(avgWatts: Double): Int      // round half up of 0.95 x avg
    fun isValid(ftp: Int?): Boolean                          // in 50..600
}
class PowerSmoother(windowMs: Long = 3_000L) { fun add(nowMs: Long, watts: Int); fun value(nowMs: Long): Int?; fun reset() }
enum class TargetStatus { BELOW, IN, ABOVE }
class TargetStatusTracker(debounceMs: Long = 2_000L, toleranceWatts: Int = 3) {
    fun update(nowMs: Long, watts: Int?, range: ZoneRange?): TargetStatus?; fun reset()
}
```

**Rules (copied from the spec):** percents 55/75/90/105/120/150. `bound_i = (pct_i * ftp + 99) / 100` in integer math (integer ceil). Zone `i` covers `[bound_{i-1}, bound_i - 1]`, Z1 low is 0, Z7 is `>= bound_6` (high = null). `zoneFor(p)` returns the first zone `i` (1..6) with `p < bound_i`, else Z7. For tiny FTPs some ranges are legitimately empty (`high < low`); never throw, never divide.

```kotlin
class PowerSmootherTest {
    @Test fun `empty smoother has no value`() = assertNull(PowerSmoother().value(0))

    @Test fun `mean over the last 3 s window`() {
        val s = PowerSmoother()
        s.add(0, 100); s.add(1_000, 200); s.add(2_000, 300)
        assertEquals(200, s.value(2_000))
        s.add(3_000, 400)                     // sample at t=0 leaves the window (now - 3000, now]
        assertEquals(300, s.value(3_000))     // mean of 200, 300, 400
    }

    @Test fun `old samples expire and an empty window is null again`() {
        val s = PowerSmoother()
        s.add(3_000, 400)
        assertEquals(400, s.value(5_001))
        assertNull(s.value(6_000))
    }

    @Test fun `mean rounds half up`() {
        val s = PowerSmoother(); s.add(0, 100); s.add(500, 101)
        assertEquals(101, s.value(500))
    }

    @Test fun `reset clears`() {
        val s = PowerSmoother(); s.add(0, 100); s.reset(); assertNull(s.value(0))
    }
}
```

`TargetStatusTrackerTest.kt` (`val z3 = ZoneTable.forFtp(200)!!.rangeOf(PowerZone.Z3)` = 150..179, `z4` = 180..209, `z7` = 300..null; `ftp1z2 = ZoneTable.forFtp(1)!!.rangeOf(PowerZone.Z2)` = empty):
- tolerance on a fresh tracker each time: watts 147 -> `IN`, 146 -> `BELOW`, 182 -> `IN`, 183 -> `ABOVE`.
- debounce: `update(0,160,z3)=IN`; `update(1000,140,z3)=IN` (pending BELOW since 1000); `update(2999,140,z3)=IN`; `update(3000,140,z3)=BELOW`.
- flicker restarts the timer: `update(0,160)=IN`, `update(1000,140)=IN`, `update(1500,160)=IN`, `update(2000,140)=IN` (pending since 2000), `update(3999,140)=IN`, `update(4000,140)=BELOW`.
- a changed target range is evaluated immediately: `update(0,160,z3)=IN`, `update(500,160,z4)=BELOW`.
- null watts returns null and resets: `update(0,140,z3)=BELOW`, `update(100,null,z3)=null`, `update(200,160,z3)=IN`.
- null range returns null; empty range (`ftp1z2`) returns null.
- open-ended Z7: `update(0,900,z7)=IN` (never ABOVE); `update(0,297,z7)=IN`, `update(0,296,z7)=BELOW` on fresh trackers.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`PowerZone.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.workout

/** Seven-zone model; [upperPercent] is the exclusive upper edge as a whole percent of FTP (null for Z7). */
enum class PowerZone(val number: Int, val upperPercent: Int?) {
    Z1(1, 55), Z2(2, 75), Z3(3, 90), Z4(4, 105), Z5(5, 120), Z6(6, 150), Z7(7, null);

    companion object {
        fun fromNumber(n: Int): PowerZone? = entries.firstOrNull { it.number == n }
    }
}
```
`ZoneTable.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.workout

data class ZoneRange(val zone: PowerZone, val lowWatts: Int, val highWatts: Int?) {
    /** True for the degenerate rows tiny FTPs produce; such a row contains no watt value. */
    val isEmpty: Boolean get() = highWatts != null && highWatts < lowWatts
}

class ZoneTable private constructor(val ftp: Int) {
    private val bounds: IntArray = IntArray(6) { i -> (PowerZone.entries[i].upperPercent!! * ftp + 99) / 100 }

    val ranges: List<ZoneRange> = PowerZone.entries.map { z ->
        val low = if (z.number == 1) 0 else bounds[z.number - 2]
        val high = if (z == PowerZone.Z7) null else bounds[z.number - 1] - 1
        ZoneRange(z, low, high)
    }

    fun rangeOf(zone: PowerZone): ZoneRange = ranges[zone.number - 1]

    fun zoneFor(watts: Int): PowerZone {
        val w = watts.coerceAtLeast(0)
        for (i in 0..5) if (w < bounds[i]) return PowerZone.entries[i]
        return PowerZone.Z7
    }

    companion object {
        private const val MAX_FTP = 1_000_000
        fun forFtp(ftp: Int?): ZoneTable? = if (ftp == null || ftp <= 0 || ftp > MAX_FTP) null else ZoneTable(ftp)
    }
}
```
`FtpCalculator.kt`: `fromTwentyMinuteAverage(avg) = kotlin.math.floor(avg * 95.0 / 100.0 + 0.5).toInt()` (do NOT use `avg * 0.95`, which is not exact), `isValid`.
`PowerSmoother.kt`: an `ArrayDeque<Pair<Long, Int>>`; `prune(now)` removes samples with `time <= now - windowMs`; `value(now)` prunes then returns null when empty else `((sum + n / 2) / n).toInt()` with `sum` as Long.
`TargetStatus.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.workout

enum class TargetStatus { BELOW, IN, ABOVE }

/** Below/in/above the target range with +-[toleranceWatts] slack; a change is shown only after [debounceMs] of agreement. */
class TargetStatusTracker(private val debounceMs: Long = 2_000L, private val toleranceWatts: Int = 3) {
    private var range: ZoneRange? = null
    private var shown: TargetStatus? = null
    private var pending: TargetStatus? = null
    private var pendingSince = 0L

    fun update(nowMs: Long, watts: Int?, range: ZoneRange?): TargetStatus? {
        if (watts == null || range == null || range.isEmpty) { reset(); return null }
        if (range != this.range) { this.range = range; shown = null; pending = null }   // new target: judge immediately
        val raw = when {
            watts < range.lowWatts - toleranceWatts -> TargetStatus.BELOW
            range.highWatts != null && watts > range.highWatts + toleranceWatts -> TargetStatus.ABOVE
            else -> TargetStatus.IN
        }
        val current = shown
        if (current == null) { shown = raw; return raw }
        if (raw == current) { pending = null; return current }
        if (pending != raw) { pending = raw; pendingSince = nowMs }
        if (nowMs - pendingSince >= debounceMs) { shown = raw; pending = null }
        return shown
    }

    fun reset() { range = null; shown = null; pending = null }
}
```

**Goal:** `@Serializable` workout model with validation, a repository that loads workouts from assets through an injectable reader, four preset workouts, and the schema documented in the spec.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/workout/WorkoutDefinition.kt`, `WorkoutJson.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/data/ContextAssetReader.kt`
- Create: `app/src/main/assets/workouts/intro-20.json`, `endurance-z2-45.json`, `pz-43.json`, `ftp-test.json`; delete `app/src/main/assets/workouts/.gitkeep` (`git rm -f`)
- Create: `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/workout/WorkoutDefinitionTest.kt`, `WorkoutRepositoryTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (A4): `PowerZone` (only for `fromNumber` in later tasks; validation uses the number range 1..7).
- Produces (package `dev.pedalmate.workout`):
```kotlin
@Serializable data class WorkoutStep(val label: String, val seconds: Int, val zone: Int? = null, val tag: String? = null)
@Serializable data class WorkoutDefinition(val id: String, val name: String, val description: String? = null, val steps: List<WorkoutStep>) {
    val totalSeconds: Int
    fun validate(): List<String>            // empty when valid
}
class WorkoutFormatException(message: String, cause: Throwable? = null) : Exception(message, cause)
object WorkoutJson { fun parse(text: String): WorkoutDefinition; fun encode(def: WorkoutDefinition): String }   // parse validates, throws WorkoutFormatException
interface AssetReader { fun list(dir: String): List<String>; fun read(path: String): String }
class WorkoutRepository(assets: AssetReader, dir: String = "workouts") {
    class LoadResult(val workouts: List<WorkoutDefinition>, val errors: Map<String, String>)
    fun load(): LoadResult; fun all(): List<WorkoutDefinition>; fun byId(id: String): WorkoutDefinition?
}
// dev.pedalmate.data.ContextAssetReader(assets: android.content.res.AssetManager) : AssetReader
```
(`ContextAssetReader` lives in `dev.pedalmate.data` so the `workout` package stays free of Android imports.)

**Schema and validation rules:**
- `id`: `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,39}$`; `name`: non-blank, at most 60 chars; `description` optional; `steps`: 1..200 entries.
- step `label`: non-blank, at most 40 chars; `seconds`: 1..21600; `zone`: omitted/null (no target, used by the FTP-test window) or 1..7; `tag`: omitted or non-blank at most 20 chars (the value `"ftp-window"` marks the 20-minute test window for Phase B).
- Unknown JSON keys are ignored; missing required keys are an error. Zone is NOT inferred from the label.

```kotlin
private class MemAssets(val files: Map<String, String>) : AssetReader {
    override fun list(dir: String) = files.keys.filter { it.startsWith("$dir/") }.map { it.removePrefix("$dir/") }
    override fun read(path: String) = files.getValue(path)
}
private class DirAssets(val root: java.io.File) : AssetReader {   // reads the real preset files; Gradle runs unit tests with cwd = app/
    override fun list(dir: String) = root.list()!!.toList()
    override fun read(path: String) = java.io.File(root, path.substringAfter("/")).readText()
}
```
- `load()` returns workouts sorted by file name; an invalid file lands in `errors` (key = file name) and does not stop the others; non-`.json` files are ignored; `byId("missing")` is null.
- presets, using `DirAssets(java.io.File("src/main/assets/workouts"))`: all four load with no errors; each preset id equals its file name without `.json`; `totalSeconds` is `intro-20` 1200, `endurance-z2-45` 2700, `pz-43` 2580, `ftp-test` 2100; `pz-43` zone sequence is `[2,3,4,3,4,5,2,1]` with labels `Warmup, Zone 3, Zone 4, Zone 3, Zone 4, Zone 5, Zone 2, Cooldown`; `ftp-test` has exactly one step tagged `ftp-window`, 1200 s, zone null.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`WorkoutDefinition.kt`: the two `@Serializable` data classes exactly as in Interfaces; `totalSeconds = steps.sumOf { it.seconds }`; `validate()` returns a `List<String>` of human-readable errors applying every rule above (collect all, do not stop at the first). `WorkoutFormatException` in the same file.
`WorkoutJson.kt`:
```kotlin
object WorkoutJson {
    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true; prettyPrint = true; encodeDefaults = false }

    fun parse(text: String): WorkoutDefinition {
        val def = try { json.decodeFromString<WorkoutDefinition>(text) }
        catch (e: SerializationException) { throw WorkoutFormatException("Invalid workout JSON: ${e.message}", e) }
        catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) { throw WorkoutFormatException("Invalid workout JSON: ${e.message}", e) }
        val errors = def.validate()
        if (errors.isNotEmpty()) throw WorkoutFormatException(errors.joinToString("; "))
        return def
    }

    fun encode(def: WorkoutDefinition): String = json.encodeToString(def)
}
```
`WorkoutRepository.kt`: `AssetReader`, `LoadResult`, and the class: `load()` lists `dir`, keeps names ending `.json` sorted ascending, parses each with `WorkoutJson.parse(assets.read("$dir/$name"))`, puts failures into `errors[name] = message`, and logs them with `Log`-free code (no Android import: collect errors only; callers log). Cache the result in a `by lazy`; `all()` and `byId()` use the cache.
`data/ContextAssetReader.kt`: `list(dir) = assets.list(dir)?.toList().orEmpty()`, `read(path) = assets.open(path).bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }`.

Preset files (write exactly; one step per line is fine):

```json
    { "label": "Zone 3",   "seconds": 300, "zone": 3 },
    { "label": "Zone 4",   "seconds": 300, "zone": 4 },
    { "label": "Zone 5",   "seconds": 180, "zone": 5 },
    { "label": "Zone 2",   "seconds": 300, "zone": 2 },
    { "label": "Cooldown", "seconds": 300, "zone": 1 }
  ]
}
```
`intro-20.json`: id `intro-20`, name `Intro 20`, steps `Warmup 300 z2`, `Zone 3 180 z3`, `Zone 2 120 z2`, `Zone 3 180 z3`, `Zone 2 120 z2`, `Zone 4 120 z4`, `Cooldown 180 z1` (sum 1200).
`endurance-z2-45.json`: id `endurance-z2-45`, name `Endurance Z2 45`, steps `Warmup 300 z1`, `Zone 2 2100 z2`, `Cooldown 300 z1` (sum 2700).
`ftp-test.json`: id `ftp-test`, name `FTP Test`, description "20-minute test window; average power x 0.95 = FTP.", steps `Warmup 600 z2`, `FTP Test 1200` with NO zone and `"tag": "ftp-window"`, `Cooldown 300 z1` (sum 2100). Playable as an ordinary workout in Phase A.

### Task A6: WorkoutEngine

**Goal:** Pure-Kotlin interval engine driven by `advance(deltaMs)`; no wall clock, no Android. Emits state and events exactly as specified below.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/workout/WorkoutEngine.kt`
- Create: `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/workout/WorkoutEngineTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (A4, A5): `ZoneTable`, `ZoneRange`, `PowerZone`, `WorkoutDefinition`, `WorkoutStep`.
- Produces (package `dev.pedalmate.workout`):
```kotlin
enum class WorkoutPhase { IDLE, RUNNING, PAUSED, FINISHED }
data class StepInfo(val index: Int, val label: String, val zone: PowerZone?, val wattRange: ZoneRange?, val seconds: Int)
data class WorkoutState(
    val phase: WorkoutPhase, val stepIndex: Int, val stepCount: Int,
    val stepElapsedMs: Long, val stepRemainingMs: Long,
    val totalElapsedMs: Long, val totalRemainingMs: Long, val totalMs: Long,
    val progress: Float, val currentStep: StepInfo?, val nextStep: StepInfo?,
)
sealed interface WorkoutEvent {
    data class Started(val first: StepInfo) : WorkoutEvent
    data class IntervalChanged(val from: StepInfo, val to: StepInfo) : WorkoutEvent
    data class Countdown(val seconds: Int) : WorkoutEvent        // 3, 2 or 1
    data class Skipped(val fromIndex: Int) : WorkoutEvent
    data object Finished : WorkoutEvent
}
class WorkoutEngine(definition: WorkoutDefinition, zoneTable: ZoneTable?) {
    val state: StateFlow<WorkoutState>
    val events: SharedFlow<WorkoutEvent>     // replay 0, extraBufferCapacity 512, tryEmit
    fun start(); fun pause(); fun resume(); fun skip(); fun advance(deltaMs: Long)
}
```

**Semantics (every one has a test below):**
- `start()` only from IDLE: phase RUNNING, emits `Started(first)`. (The first interval produces `Started`, not `IntervalChanged`.)
- `advance(deltaMs)` is ignored unless RUNNING or when `deltaMs <= 0`. It consumes time step by step: if the delta is `>=` the remaining time of the current step the step completes (leftover delta carries into the next step); finishing the last step emits `Finished`. A huge delta therefore emits every `IntervalChanged` in order, then `Finished`.
- Countdown: only for a step with `seconds >= 4` that has a next step. After an `advance` that did NOT cross a step boundary, with `before`/`after` the remaining ms of the step before/after the delta (`after > 0`), emit `Countdown(n)` for the smallest `n` in 1..3 with `before > n*1000 && after <= n*1000`; at most one Countdown per advance. An advance that crossed a step boundary emits no Countdown (stale beeps after a stalled ticker).
- `pause()` RUNNING to PAUSED, `resume()` PAUSED to RUNNING; no events. Time never advances while paused.
- `skip()` from RUNNING or PAUSED: emits `Skipped(currentIndex)`, then `IntervalChanged(from, to)` (elapsed in the new step = 0, `totalElapsedMs` = start of the new step) or, on the last step, `Finished`. A skip while PAUSED leaves the engine PAUSED unless it finishes.
- IDLE state: `currentStep = null`, `nextStep = steps[0]`, `stepRemainingMs = steps[0]` length. FINISHED state: `currentStep = null`, `nextStep = null`, `stepIndex = lastIndex`, remaining 0, `progress = 1f`.
- `StepInfo.zone = PowerZone.fromNumber(step.zone)`; `wattRange = zoneTable?.rangeOf(zone)` (null when the step has no zone or no FTP).
- `progress = totalElapsedMs / totalMs`, never decreasing. Methods are `@Synchronized` (ticker thread and UI both call).

```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.workout

import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineStart
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.TestScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.UnconfinedTestDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import org.junit.Assert.*
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class WorkoutEngineTest {
    private val ftp200 = ZoneTable.forFtp(200)
    private fun def(vararg s: WorkoutStep) = WorkoutDefinition("t", "T", null, s.toList())
    /** 27 s total: Warm 10 s Z2, Hard 8 s Z4, Short 3 s Z3, Cool 6 s Z1. */
    private val four = def(
        WorkoutStep("Warm", 10, 2), WorkoutStep("Hard", 8, 4),
        WorkoutStep("Short", 3, 3), WorkoutStep("Cool", 6, 1),
    )

    private fun WorkoutEvent.tag() = when (this) {
        is WorkoutEvent.Started -> "S"
        is WorkoutEvent.IntervalChanged -> "I${from.index}>${to.index}"
        is WorkoutEvent.Countdown -> "C$seconds"
        is WorkoutEvent.Skipped -> "K$fromIndex"
        WorkoutEvent.Finished -> "F"
    }

    private fun TestScope.record(engine: WorkoutEngine): List<String> {
        val out = mutableListOf<String>()
        backgroundScope.launch(start = CoroutineStart.UNDISPATCHED) { engine.events.collect { out += it.tag() } }
        return out
    }

    private fun WorkoutEngine.tick(totalMs: Long, stepMs: Long = 250) {
        var left = totalMs
        while (left > 0) { val d = minOf(stepMs, left); advance(d); left -= d }
    }

    @Test fun `start emits Started and runs step 0`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {
        val e = WorkoutEngine(four, ftp200)
        val events = record(e)
        assertEquals(WorkoutPhase.IDLE, e.state.value.phase)
        assertNull(e.state.value.currentStep)
        assertEquals("Warm", e.state.value.nextStep!!.label)
        e.start()
        val s = e.state.value
        assertEquals(listOf("S"), events)
        assertEquals(WorkoutPhase.RUNNING, s.phase)
        assertEquals(0, s.stepIndex); assertEquals(4, s.stepCount)
        assertEquals(10_000L, s.stepRemainingMs); assertEquals(27_000L, s.totalMs)
        assertEquals(ftp200!!.rangeOf(PowerZone.Z2), s.currentStep!!.wattRange)   // 110..149
        assertEquals("Hard", s.nextStep!!.label)
    }

    @Test fun `advance moves elapsed and remaining`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {
        val e = WorkoutEngine(four, ftp200); e.start(); e.advance(4_000)
        val s = e.state.value
        assertEquals(4_000L, s.stepElapsedMs); assertEquals(6_000L, s.stepRemainingMs)
        assertEquals(4_000L, s.totalElapsedMs); assertEquals(23_000L, s.totalRemainingMs)
    }

    @Test fun `countdown fires at 3 2 1 then the interval changes`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {
        val e = WorkoutEngine(four, ftp200); val events = record(e)
        e.start(); e.tick(10_000)
        assertEquals(listOf("S", "C3", "C2", "C1", "I0>1"), events)
    }

    @Test fun `short steps and the last step never count down`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {
        val e = WorkoutEngine(four, ftp200); val events = record(e)
        e.start(); e.tick(27_000)
        assertEquals(
            listOf("S", "C3", "C2", "C1", "I0>1", "C3", "C2", "C1", "I1>2", "I2>3", "F"), events)
    }

    @Test fun `a 4 second step still counts down`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {

        val events = record(e); e.start(); e.tick(8_000)

    @Test fun `pause freezes time and resume continues`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {

    @Test fun `huge delta emits every IntervalChanged in order and no countdown`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {

    @Test fun `stalled ticker lands mid-step without stale countdowns`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {
        val e = WorkoutEngine(four, ftp200); val events = record(e)
        e.start(); e.advance(25_000)
        assertEquals(listOf("S", "I0>1", "I1>2", "I2>3"), events)
        val s = e.state.value
        assertEquals(3, s.stepIndex); assertEquals(4_000L, s.stepElapsedMs); assertEquals(2_000L, s.stepRemainingMs)

    @Test fun `a long gap inside one step emits only the nearest countdown`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {
        val e = WorkoutEngine(four, ftp200); val events = record(e)

    @Test fun `zero negative and idle deltas are ignored`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {

    @Test fun `a step without a zone has neither zone nor range`() = runTest(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `WorkoutEngine.kt`**

```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.workout

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

// (enum WorkoutPhase, data classes StepInfo / WorkoutState and sealed interface WorkoutEvent exactly as in Interfaces)

class WorkoutEngine(definition: WorkoutDefinition, zoneTable: ZoneTable?) {
    private val steps: List<StepInfo> = definition.steps.mapIndexed { i, s ->
        val zone = s.zone?.let { PowerZone.fromNumber(it) }
        StepInfo(i, s.label, zone, if (zone != null && zoneTable != null) zoneTable.rangeOf(zone) else null, s.seconds)
    }
    private val stepMs: List<Long> = steps.map { it.seconds * 1_000L }
    private val startsAtMs: List<Long> = stepMs.runningFold(0L) { a, b -> a + b }.dropLast(1)
    private val totalMs: Long = stepMs.sum()
    private val last = steps.lastIndex

    private var phase = WorkoutPhase.IDLE
    private var index = 0
    private var elapsedInStep = 0L

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(snapshot())
    val state: StateFlow<WorkoutState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    private val _events = MutableSharedFlow<WorkoutEvent>(extraBufferCapacity = 512)
    val events: SharedFlow<WorkoutEvent> = _events.asSharedFlow()

    @Synchronized fun start() {
        if (phase != WorkoutPhase.IDLE) return
        phase = WorkoutPhase.RUNNING
        _events.tryEmit(WorkoutEvent.Started(steps[0]))
        publish()
    }

    @Synchronized fun pause() { if (phase == WorkoutPhase.RUNNING) { phase = WorkoutPhase.PAUSED; publish() } }
    @Synchronized fun resume() { if (phase == WorkoutPhase.PAUSED) { phase = WorkoutPhase.RUNNING; publish() } }

    @Synchronized fun skip() {
        if (phase != WorkoutPhase.RUNNING && phase != WorkoutPhase.PAUSED) return
        _events.tryEmit(WorkoutEvent.Skipped(index))
        if (index == last) finish() else moveToNext()
        publish()
    }

    @Synchronized fun advance(deltaMs: Long) {
        if (phase != WorkoutPhase.RUNNING || deltaMs <= 0) return
        var left = deltaMs
        var crossed = false
        while (left > 0 && phase == WorkoutPhase.RUNNING) {
            val remaining = stepMs[index] - elapsedInStep
            if (left < remaining) {
                elapsedInStep += left
                if (!crossed) maybeCountdown(before = remaining, after = remaining - left)
                left = 0
            } else {
                left -= remaining
                crossed = true
                if (index == last) finish() else moveToNext()
            }
        }
        publish()
    }

    private fun moveToNext() {
        val from = steps[index]
        index++
        elapsedInStep = 0
        _events.tryEmit(WorkoutEvent.IntervalChanged(from, steps[index]))
    }

    private fun finish() {
        phase = WorkoutPhase.FINISHED
        _events.tryEmit(WorkoutEvent.Finished)
    }

    private fun maybeCountdown(before: Long, after: Long) {
        if (steps[index].seconds < 4 || index == last) return
        for (n in 1..3) {
            if (before > n * 1_000L && after <= n * 1_000L) { _events.tryEmit(WorkoutEvent.Countdown(n)); return }
        }
    }

    private fun publish() { _state.value = snapshot() }

    private fun snapshot(): WorkoutState = when (phase) {
        WorkoutPhase.IDLE -> WorkoutState(phase, 0, steps.size, 0, stepMs[0], 0, totalMs, totalMs, 0f, null, steps[0])
        WorkoutPhase.FINISHED -> WorkoutState(phase, last, steps.size, stepMs[last], 0, totalMs, 0, totalMs, 1f, null, null)
        else -> {
            val total = startsAtMs[index] + elapsedInStep
            WorkoutState(
                phase, index, steps.size, elapsedInStep, stepMs[index] - elapsedInStep,
                total, totalMs - total, totalMs, total.toFloat() / totalMs, steps[index], steps.getOrNull(index + 1),
            )
        }
    }
}
```
(`snapshot()` is called from the property initialiser, so declare `phase`, `index`, `elapsedInStep`, `steps`, `stepMs`, `startsAtMs`, `totalMs`, `last` above `_state`, as shown. The definition is guaranteed non-empty by A5 validation; add `require(definition.steps.isNotEmpty())` in `init`.)

### Task A7: Audio cues

**Goal:** Map workout events to cues (`CuePolicy`, pure), describe each cue as a tone sequence (`CueTones`, pure), and play them on `STREAM_MUSIC` with `ToneGenerator` (`CuePlayer`). Harder next zone and easier next zone sound different.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/audio/Cue.kt`, `CuePolicy.kt`, `CueTones.kt`, `CuePlayer.kt`
- Create: `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/audio/CuePolicyTest.kt`, `CueTonesTest.kt`
- Modify: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/debug/SensorDebugScreen.kt` (add `CueDebugRow`), `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/SetupActivity.kt` (own a `CuePlayer`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (A6): `WorkoutEvent`, `StepInfo`, `PowerZone`.
- Produces (package `dev.pedalmate.audio`):
```kotlin
enum class Cue { START, COUNTDOWN, STEP_HARDER, STEP_EASIER, STEP_SAME, FINISH }
fun interface CueSink { fun play(cue: Cue) }
object CuePolicy { fun cueFor(event: WorkoutEvent): Cue? }
data class Tone(val type: Int, val durationMs: Int)
object CueTones { fun sequenceFor(cue: Cue): List<Tone> }
class CuePlayer(scope: CoroutineScope) : CueSink { override fun play(cue: Cue); fun release() }
@Composable fun CueDebugRow(onPlay: (Cue) -> Unit, onPlayDelayed: (Cue) -> Unit)   // ui.debug
```

**Policy:** `Started` -> `START`; `Countdown(n)` (3, 2, 1) -> `COUNTDOWN`; `IntervalChanged(from, to)`: both zones non-null and `to.number > from.number` -> `STEP_HARDER`, `<` -> `STEP_EASIER`, equal -> `STEP_SAME`; either zone null -> `STEP_SAME` (null zone is neutral); `Skipped` -> null (the following IntervalChanged or Finished cues); `Finished` -> `FINISH`.

**Tone table (pin it):** `START` = `[(TONE_PROP_ACK, 300)]`; `COUNTDOWN` = `[(TONE_DTMF_5, 120)]`; `STEP_HARDER` = `[(TONE_DTMF_5, 150), (TONE_DTMF_9, 250)]` (rising); `STEP_EASIER` = `[(TONE_DTMF_9, 150), (TONE_DTMF_5, 250)]` (falling); `STEP_SAME` = `[(TONE_DTMF_5, 300)]`; `FINISH` = `[(TONE_PROP_ACK, 200), (TONE_PROP_BEEP2, 400)]`. All are `android.media.ToneGenerator` constants (compile-time ints, usable in JVM tests).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

- `Started(first)` -> `START`; `Countdown(3)`, `Countdown(2)`, `Countdown(1)` -> `COUNTDOWN`.
- `IntervalChanged` Z3 to Z4 -> `STEP_HARDER`; Z4 to Z3 -> `STEP_EASIER`; Z3 to Z3 -> `STEP_SAME`; null to Z3, Z3 to null, null to null -> `STEP_SAME`; Z1 to Z7 -> `STEP_HARDER`.
- `Skipped(0)` -> null; `Finished` -> `FINISH`.

`CueTonesTest.kt`:
- every `Cue` has a non-empty sequence and positive durations;
- `CueTones.sequenceFor(STEP_HARDER).map { it.type } == CueTones.sequenceFor(STEP_EASIER).map { it.type }.reversed()` and the two lists of types are not equal;
- `STEP_SAME` types differ from `STEP_HARDER` types; `COUNTDOWN` has exactly one tone.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`Cue.kt`, `CuePolicy.kt` (a `when` over `WorkoutEvent`), `CueTones.kt` (a `when` over `Cue` using the table above and `android.media.ToneGenerator.TONE_*`).
`CuePlayer.kt`:
```kotlin
class CuePlayer(private val scope: CoroutineScope) : CueSink {
    private var generator: ToneGenerator? = null
    private var job: Job? = null

    override fun play(cue: Cue) {
        Log.i("PedalMate", "cue $cue")
        synchronized(this) {
            job?.cancel()          // a new cue pre-empts the old sequence
            job = scope.launch(Dispatchers.Default) {
                val gen = obtain() ?: return@launch
                for (tone in CueTones.sequenceFor(cue)) {
                    gen.startTone(tone.type, tone.durationMs)
                    delay(tone.durationMs + 60L)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Synchronized private fun obtain(): ToneGenerator? = generator ?: try {
        ToneGenerator(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, 100).also { generator = it }
    } catch (e: RuntimeException) {
        Log.w("PedalMate", "ToneGenerator unavailable", e); null
    }

    fun release() {
        synchronized(this) { job?.cancel(); job = null; generator?.release(); generator = null }
    }
}
```
`CueDebugRow`: a `Row` of six small buttons (`START`, `COUNTDOWN`, `HARDER`, `EASIER`, `SAME`, `FINISH`) calling `onPlay`, plus a seventh button "HARDER in 5 s" calling `onPlayDelayed(Cue.STEP_HARDER)` (lets you switch to another app before the sound). In `SetupActivity` create `val cuePlayer = CuePlayer(lifecycleScope)`, pass `extra = { CueDebugRow(onPlay = cuePlayer::play, onPlayDelayed = { cue -> lifecycleScope.launch { delay(5_000); cuePlayer.play(cue) } }) }` to `SensorDebugScreen`, and `cuePlayer.release()` in `onDestroy`.
