- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the pure/session code**

`RideSnapshot.kt`: the enum and data class from Interfaces; `idle(ftp)` returns `RideSnapshot(RideStatus.IDLE, null, ConnectionState.Unavailable, ConnectionState.Unavailable, null, null, null, null, null, ftp, null, null, null, null, null, 0L)`.

`SensorHub.kt`:
```kotlin
class SensorHub(val bike: BoundBikeDataSource, val hr: ManagedHeartRateDataSource) {
    private var holders = 0
    /** First holder starts both sensors; the Activity debug view and a ride can share them. */
    @Synchronized fun acquire() { if (holders++ == 0) { bike.start(); hr.start() } }
    @Synchronized fun release() { if (holders > 0 && --holders == 0) { bike.stop(); hr.stop() } }
}
```
`RideSession.kt` (complete):
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.ride

import android.util.Log
import dev.pedalmate.audio.CuePolicy
import dev.pedalmate.audio.CueSink
import dev.pedalmate.sensor.ConnectionState
import dev.pedalmate.workout.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineStart
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

sealed interface StartResult {
    data object Started : StartResult
    data object UnknownWorkout : StartResult
    data object AlreadyRunning : StartResult
}

class RideSession(
    private val hub: SensorHub,
    private val workouts: WorkoutRepository,
    private val rideLog: RideLog,
    private val cues: CueSink,
    private val ftpProvider: () -> Int?,
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
    private val ready: suspend () -> Unit = {},
) {
    private val _snapshot = MutableStateFlow(RideSnapshot.idle(ftpProvider()))
    val snapshot: StateFlow<RideSnapshot> = _snapshot.asStateFlow()
    private val _events = MutableSharedFlow<WorkoutEvent>(extraBufferCapacity = 64)
    val events: SharedFlow<WorkoutEvent> = _events.asSharedFlow()

    private var status = RideStatus.IDLE
    private var starting = false
    private var holdsHub = false
    private var engine: WorkoutEngine? = null
    private var workoutName: String? = null
    private var zoneTable: ZoneTable? = null
    private var rideFtp: Int? = null
    private var lastTickMs: Long? = null
    private var freeElapsedMs = 0L
    private var cueJob: Job? = null
    private val smoother = PowerSmoother()
    private val tracker = TargetStatusTracker()

    val isActive: Boolean
        @Synchronized get() = status == RideStatus.RUNNING || status == RideStatus.PAUSED

    suspend fun startWorkout(id: String): StartResult {
        val def = workouts.byId(id) ?: return StartResult.UnknownWorkout
        return begin(def)
    }

    suspend fun startFreeRide(): StartResult = begin(null)

    private suspend fun begin(def: WorkoutDefinition?): StartResult {
        val wasFinished: Boolean
        synchronized(this) {
            if (starting || status == RideStatus.RUNNING || status == RideStatus.PAUSED) return StartResult.AlreadyRunning
            starting = true
            wasFinished = status == RideStatus.FINISHED
        }
        try {
            if (wasFinished) stop()
            ready()
            val ftp = ftpProvider()
            try {
                rideLog.begin(def?.id, ftp)
            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "ride log unavailable; riding unrecorded", e)
            }
            hub.acquire()
            synchronized(this) {
                holdsHub = true
                rideFtp = ftp
                zoneTable = ZoneTable.forFtp(ftp)
                workoutName = def?.name
                smoother.reset(); tracker.reset()
                lastTickMs = null; freeElapsedMs = 0L
                cueJob?.cancel(); cueJob = null
                engine = null
                if (def != null) {
                    val e = WorkoutEngine(def, zoneTable)
                    engine = e
                    cueJob = scope.launch(start = CoroutineStart.UNDISPATCHED) {
                        e.events.collect { ev ->
                            _events.tryEmit(ev)
                            CuePolicy.cueFor(ev)?.let { cues.play(it) }
                        }
                    }
                    e.start()
                }
                status = RideStatus.RUNNING
                republish()
            }
            return StartResult.Started
        } finally {
            synchronized(this) { starting = false }
        }
    }

    @Synchronized fun pause() {
        val e = engine ?: return
        if (status != RideStatus.RUNNING) return
        e.pause(); status = RideStatus.PAUSED; republish()
    }

    @Synchronized fun resume() {
        val e = engine ?: return
        if (status != RideStatus.PAUSED) return
        e.resume(); status = RideStatus.RUNNING; republish()
    }

    @Synchronized fun skip() {
        val e = engine ?: return
        if (status == RideStatus.RUNNING || status == RideStatus.PAUSED) { e.skip(); republish() }
    }

    @Synchronized fun tick(nowMs: Long) {
        if (status == RideStatus.IDLE) return
        val last = lastTickMs
        lastTickMs = nowMs
        val delta = if (last == null) 0L else (nowMs - last).coerceAtLeast(0L)
        val e = engine
        if (status == RideStatus.RUNNING && delta > 0) {
            if (e != null) e.advance(delta) else freeElapsedMs += delta
        }
        val live = hub.bike.connectionState.value == ConnectionState.Connected
        if (live) smoother.add(nowMs, hub.bike.metrics.value.powerWatts) else smoother.reset()
        if (status != RideStatus.FINISHED && e?.state?.value?.phase == WorkoutPhase.FINISHED) {
            status = RideStatus.FINISHED
            scope.launch { safeFinish() }
        }
        val snap = buildSnapshot(nowMs)
        if (status == RideStatus.RUNNING) {
            rideLog.offer(RideFrame(snap.powerWatts, snap.cadenceRpm, snap.resistancePercent, snap.heartRateBpm, snap.currentZone?.number))
        }
        _snapshot.value = snap
    }

    suspend fun stop() {
        val needsFinish: Boolean
        val release: Boolean
        synchronized(this) {
            needsFinish = status == RideStatus.RUNNING || status == RideStatus.PAUSED
            release = holdsHub
            holdsHub = false
            cueJob?.cancel(); cueJob = null
            engine = null; zoneTable = null; rideFtp = null; workoutName = null
            status = RideStatus.IDLE; lastTickMs = null; freeElapsedMs = 0L
            smoother.reset(); tracker.reset()
            _snapshot.value = RideSnapshot.idle(ftpProvider())
        }
        if (release) hub.release()
        if (needsFinish) safeFinish()
    }

    private suspend fun safeFinish() {
        try { rideLog.finish() } catch (e: CancellationException) { throw e } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "ride finish failed", e) }
    }

    private fun republish() { _snapshot.value = buildSnapshot(lastTickMs) }

    private fun buildSnapshot(nowMs: Long?): RideSnapshot {
        val ws = engine?.state?.value
        val bikeState = hub.bike.connectionState.value
        val live = bikeState == ConnectionState.Connected
        val m = hub.bike.metrics.value
        val power = if (live) m.powerWatts else null
        val smoothed = if (live && nowMs != null) smoother.value(nowMs) else null
        val table = zoneTable
        val currentZone = if (table != null && smoothed != null) table.zoneFor(smoothed) else null
        val step = ws?.currentStep
        val range = step?.wattRange
        val targetStatus = if (nowMs != null) tracker.update(nowMs, smoothed, range) else null
        val hrState = hub.hr.connectionState.value
        return RideSnapshot(
            status = status, workoutName = workoutName, bikeState = bikeState, hrState = hrState,
            powerWatts = power, smoothedPowerWatts = smoothed,
            cadenceRpm = if (live) m.cadenceRpm else null,
            resistancePercent = if (live) m.resistancePercent else null,
            heartRateBpm = if (hrState == ConnectionState.Connected) hub.hr.bpm.value else null,
            ftp = rideFtp, currentZone = currentZone, targetZone = step?.zone, targetRange = range,
            targetStatus = targetStatus, workout = ws, elapsedMs = ws?.totalElapsedMs ?: freeElapsedMs,
        )
    }

    private companion object { const val TAG = "PedalMate" }
}
```
Notes for the implementer: the smoother/tracker reset on dropout happens because `smoother.reset()` runs in `tick` when `live` is false and `tracker.update(..., smoothed = null, ...)` resets itself when watts are null (A4 contract). `republish()` passes `lastTickMs` (null before the first tick) so no smoother or tracker state is touched at start. `ConnectionState` values compare by identity (`data object`).

`RideServicePolicy.kt`: `enum class ServiceAction` and `object RideServicePolicy` implementing the Rules.

`RideService.kt`:
```kotlin
class RideService : Service() {
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default)
    private var ticker: Job? = null
    private val container get() = appContainer

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(
            NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID, "Ride", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW)
        )
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        enterForeground()                         // the foreground contract comes first, whatever we decide next
        val session = container.session
        when (RideServicePolicy.decide(intent?.action, intent == null, session.isActive)) {
            ServiceAction.START -> startRide(intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_WORKOUT_ID))
            ServiceAction.CONTINUE -> ensureTicker()
            ServiceAction.STOP, ServiceAction.STOP_SELF -> stopRide()
        }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun enterForeground() {
        val stop = PendingIntent.getService(this, 0, stopIntent(this), PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE)
        val n = Notification.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher)
            .setContentTitle("PedalMate ride in progress")
            .setOngoing(true)
            .addAction(Notification.Action.Builder(null, "Stop", stop).build())
            .build()
        startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, n, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_LOCATION or ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_CONNECTED_DEVICE)
    }

    private fun startRide(workoutId: String?) {
        scope.launch {
            val s = container.session
            val result = if (workoutId == null) s.startFreeRide() else s.startWorkout(workoutId)
            Log.i("PedalMate", "start ride workout=$workoutId result=$result")
            if (result == StartResult.UnknownWorkout) stopRide() else ensureTicker()
        }
    }

    private fun stopRide() {
        container.scope.launch {                       // app scope: survives this service being destroyed
            container.session.stop()
            ticker?.cancel()
            stopForeground(true)
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    private fun ensureTicker() {
        if (ticker?.isActive == true) return
        ticker = scope.launch {
            var n = 0
            while (isActive) {
                val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
                container.session.tick(now)
                if (++n % 20 == 0) {                  // every 5 s: one line, never per frame
                    val s = container.session.snapshot.value
                    Log.i("PedalMate", "ride status=${s.status} step=${s.workout?.stepIndex} elapsed=${s.elapsedMs / 1000}s bike=${s.bikeState} watts=${s.smoothedPowerWatts} zone=${s.currentZone} target=${s.targetStatus}")
                }
                delay(TICK_MS)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        scope.cancel()                                // ticker only; the session lives on in AppContainer
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_START = "dev.pedalmate.action.START_RIDE"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "dev.pedalmate.action.STOP_RIDE"
        const val EXTRA_WORKOUT_ID = "workout_id"
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "ride"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1
        private const val TICK_MS = 250L

        fun start(context: Context, workoutId: String?) {
            val i = Intent(context, RideService::class.java).setAction(ACTION_START)
            if (workoutId != null) i.putExtra(EXTRA_WORKOUT_ID, workoutId)
            ContextCompat.startForegroundService(context, i)
        }
        fun stopIntent(context: Context): Intent = Intent(context, RideService::class.java).setAction(ACTION_STOP)
    }
}
```
(`R` is `dev.pedalmate.R`; `ServiceInfo` is `android.content.pm.ServiceInfo`. `ACTION_*` must stay `const val` so the policy test can use them without loading the service.)

`AppContainer.kt`:
```kotlin
class AppContainer(context: Context) {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext
    val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default)
    val settings: SettingsStore = SettingsStore.create(appContext)
    val database: AppDatabase by lazy { AppDatabase.create(appContext) }
    val workouts = WorkoutRepository(ContextAssetReader(appContext.assets))
    val hrStore: SavedHrDeviceStore = SettingsHrDeviceStore(settings, scope)
    val hub = SensorHub(
        bike = SensorFactory.createBikeSource(appContext, scope),
        hr = SensorFactory.createHeartRateSource(appContext, scope, hrStore),
    )
    val cuePlayer = CuePlayer(scope)

    @Volatile private var currentFtp: Int? = null
    private val ftpLoaded = CompletableDeferred<Unit>()
    private val finalizer = scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try { RideFinalizer(database.rideDao(), database.sampleDao()).finalizeUnfinished() }
        catch (e: Exception) { Log.w("PedalMate", "finalizing unfinished rides failed", e) }
    }
    init {
        scope.launch { settings.settings.collect { currentFtp = it.ftp; ftpLoaded.complete(Unit) } }
    }

    val session = RideSession(
        hub = hub, workouts = workouts,
        rideLog = RideRecorder(database.rideDao(), database.sampleDao(), { System.currentTimeMillis() }, scope),
        cues = cuePlayer, ftpProvider = { currentFtp }, scope = scope,
        ready = { finalizer.join(); ftpLoaded.await() },
    )
}
val Context.appContainer: AppContainer get() = (applicationContext as PedalMateApp).container
```
(`database` is `by lazy`, and `finalizer` and `session` reference it during construction, so it is created at app start; declaration order matters: `database`, `workouts`, `hub`, `cuePlayer`, then `finalizer`, then `session`.) `PedalMateApp : Application { lateinit var container: AppContainer; override fun onCreate() { super.onCreate(); container = AppContainer(this) } }`.

`SettingsHrDeviceStore.kt`: as designed (a `@Volatile cached`, `init` collects `settings.settings` on `Dispatchers.IO` into `cached = it.hrAddress?.let { a -> SavedHrDevice(a, it.hrName) }`, `load() = cached`, `save(d)` sets `cached = d` and launches `settings.setHrDevice(d?.address, d?.name)` on IO).

```xml
<service
    android:name=".ride.RideService"
    android:exported="false"
    android:foregroundServiceType="location|connectedDevice" />
```
(replacing the A1 comment).

`RideDebugPanel.kt` (package `dev.pedalmate.ui.debug`, temporary): a composable `RideDebugPanel(snapshot: RideSnapshot, onStartWorkout: () -> Unit, onFreeRide: () -> Unit, onPause: () -> Unit, onResume: () -> Unit, onSkip: () -> Unit, onStop: () -> Unit)` showing one small monospace text block (`status`, workout name, step label, `stepRemainingMs` as seconds, target range, `targetStatus`, `bikeState`) and a `Row` of buttons `START PZ-43`, `FREE RIDE`, `PAUSE`, `RESUME`, `SKIP`, `STOP`.

`SetupActivity.kt`: replace the A3/A7 content with: `val c = appContainer`; `onStart { c.hub.acquire() }`, `onStop { c.hub.release() }` (so the A3 sensor screen still works with nothing else running); `setContent { PedalMateTheme { val m by c.hub.bike.metrics.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(); val st by c.hub.bike.connectionState.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(); val snap by c.session.snapshot.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(); SensorDebugScreen(m, st) { CueDebugRow(onPlay = c.cuePlayer::play, onPlayDelayed = { cue -> lifecycleScope.launch { delay(5_000); c.cuePlayer.play(cue) } }); RideDebugPanel(snap, onStartWorkout = { RideService.start(applicationContext, "pz-43") }, onFreeRide = { RideService.start(applicationContext, null) }, onPause = c.session::pause, onResume = c.session::resume, onSkip = c.session::skip, onStop = { startService(RideService.stopIntent(this@SetupActivity)) }) } } }`. Remove the A7 per-activity `CuePlayer` and its `onDestroy` release (the container owns it). Temporary debug hook (A14 removes it): in `onCreate`, `intent.getIntExtra("debug_ftp", 0).takeIf { it > 0 }?.let { f -> lifecycleScope.launch { c.settings.setFtp(f) } }`, so the emulator can set an FTP without UI (`adb shell am start -n dev.pedalmate.mock/dev.pedalmate.ui.setup.SetupActivity --ei debug_ftp 250`; `setFtp` rejects values outside 50..600 by throwing, which is fine for a debug hook but wrap the call in `try/catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) { Log.w("PedalMate", "debug_ftp rejected", e) }`).

### Task A11: Overlay window host

**Goal:** A floating, draggable window that can host any Compose content over other apps: the pure placement maths (`OverlayGeometry`), a drag-aware container that lets taps through to Compose (`DraggableFrameLayout`), the lifecycle/savedstate/viewmodel owners Compose needs outside an Activity (`ComposeOverlayHost`), a guarded `addView` wrapper (`OverlayWindow`) and the panel controller (`OverlayController`). Nothing in the ride shows it yet (A12 does); a temporary test overlay in `SetupActivity` proves it on the emulator.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/overlay/OverlayGeometry.kt`, `DraggableFrameLayout.kt`, `ComposeOverlayHost.kt`, `OverlayWindow.kt`, `OverlayController.kt`
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/debug/OverlayTestRow.kt` (temporary; A12 removes it)
- Modify: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/SetupActivity.kt`
- Create (tests): `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/overlay/OverlayGeometryTest.kt`, `DraggableFrameLayoutTest.kt`, `ComposeOverlayHostTest.kt`, `OverlayControllerTest.kt`; `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/testutil/FakeWindowManager.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: A1 build setup (Compose, Robolectric, `PedalMateTheme`).
- Produces:
```kotlin
// dev.pedalmate.overlay
data class OverlayPosition(val x: Int, val y: Int)
object OverlayGeometry {
    const val SNAP_THRESHOLD_PX = 48; const val DEFAULT_TOP_MARGIN_PX = 80
    fun clampToScreen(pos: OverlayPosition, viewW: Int, viewH: Int, screenW: Int, screenH: Int): OverlayPosition
    fun snapToEdge(pos: OverlayPosition, viewW: Int, screenW: Int, thresholdPx: Int = SNAP_THRESHOLD_PX): OverlayPosition
    fun defaultPosition(viewW: Int, screenW: Int, topMarginPx: Int = DEFAULT_TOP_MARGIN_PX): OverlayPosition
    fun reanchor(prev: OverlayPosition, prevW: Int, newW: Int, screenW: Int): OverlayPosition
}
class DraggableFrameLayout(context: Context, onDrag: (dx: Int, dy: Int) -> Unit, onDragEnd: () -> Unit) : FrameLayout
class ComposeOverlayHost(context: Context, onDrag: (Int, Int) -> Unit, onDragEnd: () -> Unit, content: @Composable () -> Unit) : LifecycleOwner, ViewModelStoreOwner, SavedStateRegistryOwner {
    val root: DraggableFrameLayout; val composeView: ComposeView
    fun onAttached()      // lifecycle -> RESUMED (call right after addView)
    fun destroy()         // lifecycle -> DESTROYED, then viewModelStore.clear()
}
enum class ShowResult { SHOWN, ALREADY_SHOWN, NO_PERMISSION, FAILED }
class OverlayWindow(windowManager: WindowManager, canDraw: () -> Boolean) {
    val isShowing: Boolean
    fun show(host: ComposeOverlayHost, params: WindowManager.LayoutParams): ShowResult
    fun update(params: WindowManager.LayoutParams)
    fun hide()
}
class OverlayController(context: Context, windowManager: WindowManager, canDraw: () -> Boolean = { Settings.canDrawOverlays(context) }, screenSize: () -> Pair<Int, Int> = { displayMetrics width to height }) {
    val isShowing: Boolean
    fun show(content: @Composable () -> Unit, initial: OverlayPosition?, onMoved: (OverlayPosition) -> Unit): ShowResult
    fun hide()
    fun setKeepScreenOn(on: Boolean)     // remembered when called before show()
}
// test util: FakeWindowManager (below)
```

**Rules (spec, repeated):**
- Window: `TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY`, flags exactly `FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE` (this implies `NOT_TOUCH_MODAL`, which is what lets touches outside the panel reach the app underneath; never add `FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL` yourself, and never use `FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS`), `PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT`, width and height `WRAP_CONTENT`, gravity `TOP or START` (so `params.x/y` are absolute pixels from the top-left). `FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON` is OR-ed in only while keep-screen-on is requested.
- Owner init order is load-bearing (Compose crashes with "ViewTreeLifecycleOwner not found" otherwise): `savedStateController.performRestore(null)`, then lifecycle `CREATED`, then `ViewTreeLifecycleOwner.set / ViewTreeViewModelStoreOwner.set / ViewTreeSavedStateRegistryOwner.set` on BOTH `root` and `composeView` (the Compose recomposer looks at the window's top view, which is `root`), then `addView`, then lifecycle `RESUMED` (`onAttached`). On removal: lifecycle `DESTROYED` first, then `viewModelStore.clear()`. Use the static `ViewTree*.set(view, owner)` forms.
- `addView` is guarded: `canDraw()` false returns `NO_PERMISSION` without touching the window manager; any `RuntimeException` from `addView` (`WindowManager.BadTokenException`, `SecurityException`, ...) is caught, logged with tag `PedalMate`, the host is destroyed, and `FAILED` is returned. The service must never crash because of this.
- No `Dialog`, `Popup` or `DropdownMenu` anywhere in overlay content (they need their own windows and tokens and break here).
- Drag moves the window with raw screen coordinates (`MotionEvent.rawX/rawY`); view-relative coordinates jitter because the window moves under the finger. A touch is only taken for dragging once it has moved more than `ViewConfiguration.scaledTouchSlop`; until then it belongs to Compose, so taps toggle the panel (A12).
- Geometry: `clampToScreen` keeps `x` in `[0, max(0, screenW - viewW)]` and `y` in `[0, max(0, screenH - viewH)]`. `snapToEdge` clamps `x` first, then snaps to the left edge when `x < threshold` and to the right edge (`max(0, screenW - viewW)`) when `maxX - x < threshold`; comparisons are strict `<` and the left edge wins a tie. `defaultPosition` docks right: `x = max(0, screenW - viewW)`, `y = topMargin`. `reanchor` keeps a panel that was docked right (`prev.x == max(0, screenW - prevW)`, and that value is above 0) docked right after its width changes; any other position keeps `x`, clamped to the new `max(0, screenW - newW)`; `y` is unchanged.

`OverlayGeometryTest.kt` (pure JVM; screen 1920x1080, view 240x300, so `maxX = 1680`, `maxY = 780`):
- `clampToScreen`: `(100,100)` unchanged; `(-30, -5)` gives `(0, 0)`; `(2000, 2000)` gives `(1680, 780)`; view wider than the screen (`viewW=2000`) gives `x == 0`; view taller than the screen gives `y == 0`.
- `snapToEdge(thresholdPx = 48)`: `x=0` gives `0`; `x=47` gives `0`; `x=48` stays `48` (strict `<`); `x=1633` (distance 47 from 1680) gives `1680`; `x=1632` (distance 48) stays `1632`; `x=-30` gives `0`; `x=2000` gives `1680`; `x=900` stays `900`; `y` is never changed (`snapToEdge(OverlayPosition(900, 321), ...).y == 321`); tie: `screenW=100, viewW=40` (`maxX=60`), `x=30` gives `0` (left wins); view wider than screen: `x=500` gives `0`.
- `defaultPosition(240, 1920)` gives `(1680, 80)`; `defaultPosition(240, 1920, topMarginPx = 10)` gives `(1680, 10)`; `defaultPosition(2000, 1920)` gives `(0, 80)`.
- `reanchor`: docked right `prev = (1680, 100)`, `prevW = 240`, `newW = 120` gives `(1800, 100)`; `newW = 400` gives `(1520, 100)`; docked left `prev = (0, 100)`, `240 -> 120` gives `(0, 100)`; mid `prev = (500, 100)`, `240 -> 120` gives `(500, 100)`; mid `prev = (1000, 100)`, `240 -> 1000` gives `(920, 100)` (clamped); `prevW = 1920` (full width, `maxX = 0`, not "docked right" because the value is 0) with `newW = 240` and `prev = (0, 5)` gives `(0, 5)`.

`DraggableFrameLayoutTest.kt` (Robolectric; `@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)`; `val slop = ViewConfiguration.get(ctx).scaledTouchSlop`; helper `fun ev(action: Int, x: Float, y: Float) = MotionEvent.obtain(0L, 0L, action, x, y, 0)`):
- Tap: `onInterceptTouchEvent(DOWN 100,100)` is false; `onInterceptTouchEvent(MOVE 100 + slop/2, 100)` is false; `onInterceptTouchEvent(UP ...)` is false; `onDrag` and `onDragEnd` never called.
- Drag: DOWN `(100, 100)` is not intercepted; MOVE `(100 + slop + 5, 100)` IS intercepted (true) and `onDrag(slop + 5, 0)` was called once (the crossing move reports its whole distance from the down point so the window does not jump); then `onTouchEvent(MOVE (100 + slop + 15, 130))` calls `onDrag(10, 30)` and returns true; `onTouchEvent(UP ...)` calls `onDragEnd` once and returns true.
- After a drag ends, a new DOWN/MOVE within slop is a tap again (not intercepted).
- CANCEL during a drag also calls `onDragEnd` once.

`FakeWindowManager.kt` (test util, used again by A13):
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.testutil

import android.view.View
import android.view.WindowManager
import java.lang.reflect.InvocationHandler
import java.lang.reflect.Proxy

/** Records addView/removeView/updateViewLayout instead of touching a real window; failures can be injected. */
class FakeWindowManager {
    class Added(val view: View, val params: WindowManager.LayoutParams)
    val added = mutableListOf<Added>()
    val removed = mutableListOf<View>()
    val updates = mutableListOf<Pair<View, WindowManager.LayoutParams>>()
    var addFailure: RuntimeException? = null
    var removeFailure: RuntimeException? = null

    val manager: WindowManager = Proxy.newProxyInstance(
        WindowManager::class.java.classLoader,
        arrayOf(WindowManager::class.java),
        InvocationHandler { proxy, method, args ->
            when (method.name) {
                "addView" -> { addFailure?.let { throw it }; added += Added(args[0] as View, args[1] as WindowManager.LayoutParams); null }
                "removeView", "removeViewImmediate" -> { removeFailure?.let { throw it }; removed += args[0] as View; null }
                "updateViewLayout" -> { updates += (args[0] as View) to (args[1] as WindowManager.LayoutParams); null }
                "hashCode" -> System.identityHashCode(proxy)
                "equals" -> proxy === args[0]
                "toString" -> "FakeWindowManager"
                else -> when (method.returnType) {
                    java.lang.Integer.TYPE -> 0
                    java.lang.Boolean.TYPE -> false
                    java.lang.Float.TYPE -> 0f
                    java.lang.Long.TYPE -> 0L
                    else -> null
                }
            }
        },
    ) as WindowManager
}
```

`OverlayGeometry.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.overlay

data class OverlayPosition(val x: Int, val y: Int)

/** Pure placement maths for the overlay window (pixels, top-left origin). */
object OverlayGeometry {
    const val SNAP_THRESHOLD_PX = 48
    const val DEFAULT_TOP_MARGIN_PX = 80

    private fun maxCoord(screen: Int, view: Int) = maxOf(0, screen - view)

    fun clampToScreen(pos: OverlayPosition, viewW: Int, viewH: Int, screenW: Int, screenH: Int) = OverlayPosition(
        x = pos.x.coerceIn(0, maxCoord(screenW, viewW)),
        y = pos.y.coerceIn(0, maxCoord(screenH, viewH)),
    )

    fun snapToEdge(pos: OverlayPosition, viewW: Int, screenW: Int, thresholdPx: Int = SNAP_THRESHOLD_PX): OverlayPosition {
        val maxX = maxCoord(screenW, viewW)
        val x = pos.x.coerceIn(0, maxX)
        val snapped = when {
            x < thresholdPx -> 0
            maxX - x < thresholdPx -> maxX
            else -> x
        }
        return OverlayPosition(snapped, pos.y)
    }

    fun defaultPosition(viewW: Int, screenW: Int, topMarginPx: Int = DEFAULT_TOP_MARGIN_PX) =
        OverlayPosition(maxCoord(screenW, viewW), topMarginPx)

    fun reanchor(prev: OverlayPosition, prevW: Int, newW: Int, screenW: Int): OverlayPosition {
        val prevMax = maxCoord(screenW, prevW)
        val dockedRight = prevMax > 0 && prev.x == prevMax
        val newMax = maxCoord(screenW, newW)
        return OverlayPosition(if (dockedRight) newMax else prev.x.coerceIn(0, newMax), prev.y)
    }
}
```
`DraggableFrameLayout.kt`:
```kotlin
class DraggableFrameLayout(
    context: Context,
    private val onDrag: (dx: Int, dy: Int) -> Unit,
    private val onDragEnd: () -> Unit,
) : FrameLayout(context) {
    private val slop = ViewConfiguration.get(context).scaledTouchSlop
    private var downX = 0f; private var downY = 0f
    private var lastX = 0f; private var lastY = 0f
    private var dragging = false

    override fun onInterceptTouchEvent(ev: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        when (ev.actionMasked) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> { downX = ev.rawX; downY = ev.rawY; lastX = downX; lastY = downY; dragging = false }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> if (!dragging) {
                if (abs(ev.rawX - downX) > slop || abs(ev.rawY - downY) > slop) { dragging = true; emitDelta(ev) }
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> dragging = false
        }
        return dragging
    }

    override fun onTouchEvent(ev: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        if (!dragging) return super.onTouchEvent(ev)
        when (ev.actionMasked) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> emitDelta(ev)
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> { dragging = false; onDragEnd() }
        }
        return true
    }

    private fun emitDelta(ev: MotionEvent) {
        val dx = (ev.rawX - lastX).toInt(); val dy = (ev.rawY - lastY).toInt()
        lastX = ev.rawX; lastY = ev.rawY
        if (dx != 0 || dy != 0) onDrag(dx, dy)
    }
}
```
(Check the test numbers against this: crossing move from `100` to `100 + slop + 5` reports `slop + 5`; the next move reports 10 and 30. Truncating `toInt()` is exact for the integer-valued test events; in production sub-pixel remainders are lost per event, which is fine for a drag.)

`ComposeOverlayHost.kt`:
```kotlin
class ComposeOverlayHost(
    context: Context,
    onDrag: (Int, Int) -> Unit,
    onDragEnd: () -> Unit,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit,
) : LifecycleOwner, ViewModelStoreOwner, SavedStateRegistryOwner {
    private val lifecycleRegistry = LifecycleRegistry(this)
    private val store = ViewModelStore()
    private val savedStateController = SavedStateRegistryController.create(this)

    override val lifecycle: Lifecycle get() = lifecycleRegistry
    override val viewModelStore: ViewModelStore get() = store
    override val savedStateRegistry: SavedStateRegistry get() = savedStateController.savedStateRegistry

    val root = DraggableFrameLayout(context, onDrag, onDragEnd)
    val composeView = ComposeView(context)

    init {
        savedStateController.performRestore(null)
        lifecycleRegistry.currentState = Lifecycle.State.CREATED
        for (v in listOf<View>(root, composeView)) {
            ViewTreeLifecycleOwner.set(v, this)
            ViewTreeViewModelStoreOwner.set(v, this)
            ViewTreeSavedStateRegistryOwner.set(v, this)
        }
        composeView.setViewCompositionStrategy(ViewCompositionStrategy.DisposeOnViewTreeLifecycleDestroyed)
        composeView.setContent(content)
        root.addView(composeView)
    }

    fun onAttached() { lifecycleRegistry.currentState = Lifecycle.State.RESUMED }

    fun destroy() {
        lifecycleRegistry.currentState = Lifecycle.State.DESTROYED
        store.clear()
    }
}
```
(Do NOT call `performAttach`; `performRestore(null)` is enough for a fresh start. `LifecycleRegistry(this)` enforces the main thread: build the host only on the main thread, which the service does.)

`OverlayWindow.kt`:
```kotlin
enum class ShowResult { SHOWN, ALREADY_SHOWN, NO_PERMISSION, FAILED }

/** The one place that calls WindowManager.addView/removeView for an overlay window. Main thread only. */
class OverlayWindow(private val windowManager: WindowManager, private val canDraw: () -> Boolean) {
    private var host: ComposeOverlayHost? = null
    val isShowing: Boolean get() = host != null

    fun show(host: ComposeOverlayHost, params: WindowManager.LayoutParams): ShowResult {
        if (this.host != null) { host.destroy(); return ShowResult.ALREADY_SHOWN }
        if (!canDraw()) { host.destroy(); return ShowResult.NO_PERMISSION }
        return try {
            windowManager.addView(host.root, params)
            host.onAttached()
            this.host = host
            ShowResult.SHOWN
        } catch (e: RuntimeException) {
            Log.w(TAG, "overlay addView failed", e)
            host.destroy()
            ShowResult.FAILED
        }
    }

    fun update(params: WindowManager.LayoutParams) {
        val h = host ?: return
        try { windowManager.updateViewLayout(h.root, params) } catch (e: RuntimeException) { Log.w(TAG, "overlay update failed", e) }
    }

    fun hide() {
        val h = host ?: return
        host = null
        try { windowManager.removeViewImmediate(h.root) } catch (e: RuntimeException) { Log.w(TAG, "overlay remove failed", e) }
        h.destroy()
    }

    private companion object { const val TAG = "PedalMate" }
}
```
`OverlayController.kt`:
```kotlin
class OverlayController(
    private val context: Context,
    windowManager: WindowManager,
    canDraw: () -> Boolean = { Settings.canDrawOverlays(context) },
    private val screenSize: () -> Pair<Int, Int> = {
        val m = context.resources.displayMetrics; m.widthPixels to m.heightPixels
    },
) {
    private val window = OverlayWindow(windowManager, canDraw)
    private var params: WindowManager.LayoutParams? = null
    private var keepScreenOn = false
    private var onMoved: (OverlayPosition) -> Unit = {}
    private var placed = false          // true after the first layout decided the position
    private var hasInitial = false
    val isShowing: Boolean get() = window.isShowing

    fun show(content: @Composable () -> Unit, initial: OverlayPosition?, onMoved: (OverlayPosition) -> Unit): ShowResult {
        if (window.isShowing) return ShowResult.ALREADY_SHOWN
        this.onMoved = onMoved
        placed = false; hasInitial = initial != null
        val p = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY, flags(), PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT,
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
            x = initial?.x ?: screenSize().first      // WindowManager clamps; the first layout corrects it
            y = initial?.y ?: OverlayGeometry.DEFAULT_TOP_MARGIN_PX
        }
        val host = ComposeOverlayHost(context, ::dragBy, ::dragEnded, content)
        host.root.addOnLayoutChangeListener { _, left, top, right, bottom, oldLeft, _, oldRight, _ -> onLayout(right - left, bottom - top, oldRight - oldLeft) }
        params = p
        val result = window.show(host, p)
        if (result != ShowResult.SHOWN) params = null
        return result
    }

    fun hide() { window.hide(); params = null }

    fun setKeepScreenOn(on: Boolean) {
        if (keepScreenOn == on) return
        keepScreenOn = on
        val p = params ?: return
        p.flags = flags()
        window.update(p)
    }

    private fun flags() = WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or
        (if (keepScreenOn) WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON else 0)

    private var viewW = 0; private var viewH = 0

    private fun onLayout(w: Int, h: Int, prevW: Int) {
        val p = params ?: return
        viewW = w; viewH = h
        if (w <= 0) return
        val (sw, sh) = screenSize()
        val next = when {
            !placed -> {
                placed = true
                if (hasInitial) OverlayGeometry.clampToScreen(OverlayPosition(p.x, p.y), w, h, sw, sh)
                else OverlayGeometry.defaultPosition(w, sw)
            }
            prevW > 0 && prevW != w -> OverlayGeometry.clampToScreen(
                OverlayGeometry.reanchor(OverlayPosition(p.x, p.y), prevW, w, sw), w, h, sw, sh,
            )
            else -> return
        }
        if (next.x != p.x || next.y != p.y) { p.x = next.x; p.y = next.y; window.update(p) }
    }

    private fun dragBy(dx: Int, dy: Int) {
        val p = params ?: return
        val (sw, sh) = screenSize()
        val next = OverlayGeometry.clampToScreen(OverlayPosition(p.x + dx, p.y + dy), viewW, viewH, sw, sh)
        p.x = next.x; p.y = next.y
        window.update(p)
    }

    private fun dragEnded() {
        val p = params ?: return
        val (sw, _) = screenSize()
        val snapped = OverlayGeometry.snapToEdge(OverlayPosition(p.x, p.y), viewW, sw)
        p.x = snapped.x; p.y = snapped.y
        window.update(p)
        onMoved(snapped)
    }
}
```
Notes: in test 8 the first layout goes through `!placed` with no initial: `defaultPosition(240, 1920)` = `(1680, 80)`. In test 10 the second layout (width 240 to 120) goes through `reanchor`. `viewH` is 0 until the first layout; drag cannot start before then. In test 11 `onMoved` is only called from `dragEnded`, never from `onLayout` (test 12).

Temporary debug wiring (A12 deletes it): `OverlayTestRow(onShow, onHide)` composable with two buttons (`SHOW TEST OVERLAY`, `HIDE TEST OVERLAY`) rendered below `RideDebugPanel`. In `SetupActivity`: `private val testOverlay by lazy { OverlayController(applicationContext, applicationContext.getSystemService(WindowManager::class.java)) }`; `showTestOverlay()` calls `testOverlay.show({ Box(Modifier.background(Color(0xCC000000)).padding(24.dp)) { Text("PedalMate overlay test", color = Color.White) } }, null, { Log.i("PedalMate", "overlay moved to $it") })` and logs the `ShowResult`; `onDestroy` calls `testOverlay.hide()`. In `onCreate`, `if (intent.getBooleanExtra("show_test_overlay", false)) showTestOverlay()`.

### Task A12: Overlay UI

**Goal:** The real overlay content (expanded right-docked column and a minimized pill) driven by `RideSnapshot`, with all formatting in a pure, tested `OverlayUiModel`; the ride service shows and hides the overlay with the session and persists panel position and minimized state.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/overlay/OverlayFormat.kt`, `OverlayUiModel.kt`, `OverlayContent.kt`, `OverlayPolicy.kt`, `OverlayPrefs.kt`
- Modify: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ride/RideService.kt`; `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/SetupActivity.kt` (remove the A11 test overlay and its debug row)
- Delete: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/debug/OverlayTestRow.kt`
- Create (tests): `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/overlay/OverlayFormatTest.kt`, `OverlayUiModelTest.kt`, `OverlayPolicyTest.kt`, `OverlayPrefsTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: A10 `RideSnapshot`, `RideStatus`, `RideSession`, `RideService`, `AppContainer`; A11 `OverlayController`, `OverlayPosition`, `ShowResult`; A4 `ZoneRange`, `PowerZone`, `TargetStatus`; A6 `WorkoutState`, `StepInfo`; A8 `SettingsStore`.
- Produces:
```kotlin
// dev.pedalmate.overlay
fun formatClock(ms: Long): String
fun formatRange(range: ZoneRange?): String
data class OverlayUiModel(
    val zoneChip: String, val zone: PowerZone?, val wattsText: String, val banner: String?,
    val targetText: String?, val targetStatus: TargetStatus?, val statusText: String?,
    val intervalName: String, val intervalTime: String?, val nextText: String?,
    val hrText: String, val cadenceText: String, val resistanceText: String,
    val progress: Float, val needsFtp: Boolean, val pillText: String,
) { companion object { fun from(s: RideSnapshot): OverlayUiModel } }
@Composable fun OverlayContent(model: OverlayUiModel, minimized: Boolean, onToggle: () -> Unit)
data class OverlayDirective(val visible: Boolean, val keepScreenOn: Boolean)
object OverlayPolicy { fun directive(status: RideStatus): OverlayDirective }
data class OverlayPrefsState(val minimized: Boolean, val position: OverlayPosition?)
class OverlayPrefs(settings: SettingsStore) { suspend fun load(): OverlayPrefsState; suspend fun setMinimized(m: Boolean); suspend fun setPosition(p: OverlayPosition) }
```

**Rules:**
- `formatClock(ms)`: `m:ss`, minutes NOT zero-padded and NOT wrapped at 60, seconds rounded UP (`ceil`), negatives are `"0:00"`. `600000` gives `"10:00"`; `599999` gives `"10:00"`; `600001` gives `"10:01"`; `1` gives `"0:01"`; `0` gives `"0:00"`; `-5` gives `"0:00"`; `3725000` gives `"62:05"`; `180000` gives `"3:00"`; `59001` gives `"1:00"`; `61000` gives `"1:01"`.
- `formatRange`: null or `isEmpty` gives `"--"`; bounded gives `"<low>\u2013<high> W"` (en dash U+2013, space before `W`); open-ended (Z7) gives `"Z7 \u2265 <low> W"` (U+2265). Write these as `\u2013` / `\u2265` escapes in source, never literal characters.
- `OverlayUiModel.from(snapshot)` rules (Review Focus 2 and 3 live here):
  - `zoneChip = "Z<n>"` of `currentZone`, else `"--"`; `zone = currentZone`.
  - `wattsText = smoothedPowerWatts?.toString() ?: "--"` (the smoothed value, never the raw `powerWatts`).
  - `banner = "NO SENSOR"` when `bikeState != Connected` (Disconnected or Unavailable), else null. Because the session already nulls power/zone/status on dropout, a dropped bike also yields `"--"` for chip and watts: the numbers can never look live.
  - `targetText`: `formatRange(targetRange)` when `targetRange != null`; otherwise `"Z<n>"` of `targetZone` when only the zone is known (no FTP); otherwise null.
  - `statusText`: `BELOW` gives `"BELOW"`, `IN` gives `"IN ZONE"`, `ABOVE` gives `"ABOVE"`, null gives null; `targetStatus` is passed through for colouring.
  - `intervalName`: status `IDLE` gives `"Ready"`; `FINISHED` gives `"Done"`; free ride (`workout == null`) gives `"Free ride"`; otherwise `workout.currentStep?.label ?: "Ready"`.
  - `intervalTime`: workout with a current step gives `formatClock(workout.stepRemainingMs)`; free ride and `RUNNING`/`PAUSED` gives `formatClock(elapsedMs)` (counts up); everything else null.
  - `nextText`: workout with a current step gives `"Next: <label> <m:ss>"` of `nextStep` (`formatClock(seconds * 1000L)`), or `"Last interval"` when `nextStep == null`; otherwise null.
  - `hrText = heartRateBpm?.toString() ?: "--"`; `cadenceText = cadenceRpm?.toString() ?: "--"`; `resistanceText = resistancePercent?.let { "$it%" } ?: "--"`.
  - `progress = workout?.progress ?: 0f`; `needsFtp = ftp == null`.
  - `pillText`: bike not connected gives `"NO SENSOR"`; else `"<zoneChip>  <wattsText> W"` (two spaces), where the trailing ` W` is dropped when watts is `"--"`. Examples: `"Z4  183 W"`, `"--  183 W"` (FTP null), `"--  --"` (connected, no reading yet).
- `OverlayPolicy.directive(status)`: `visible = status != IDLE`; `keepScreenOn = status == RUNNING`.
- `OverlayPrefs.load()`: `position` is non-null only when BOTH `overlayX` and `overlayY` are stored; `minimized` defaults to false.
- Service integration: the overlay is shown while the session is not IDLE (so it stays visible on FINISHED until the rider stops), hidden on IDLE. A `NO_PERMISSION` or `FAILED` result is only logged; the ride continues. The overlay code runs on the main thread: the service gets a second scope `uiScope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Main.immediate)`, cancelled in `onDestroy`, and `onDestroy` also calls `overlay.hide()` BEFORE cancelling (the window must not leak when the service is destroyed).
- No Dialog/Popup/DropdownMenu. Tapping anywhere on the panel toggles minimized; the toggle persists.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`OverlayFormat.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.overlay

import dev.pedalmate.workout.ZoneRange

fun formatClock(ms: Long): String {
    val totalSeconds = if (ms <= 0L) 0L else (ms + 999L) / 1000L
    return "${totalSeconds / 60}:${(totalSeconds % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0')}"
}

fun formatRange(range: ZoneRange?): String = when {
    range == null || range.isEmpty -> "--"
    range.highWatts == null -> "Z${range.zone.number} \u2265 ${range.lowWatts} W"
    else -> "${range.lowWatts}\u2013${range.highWatts} W"
}
```
`OverlayUiModel.kt`: the data class from Interfaces and `from(s)` implementing the Rules exactly; `private val NO_SENSOR = "NO SENSOR"`; `bikeLive = s.bikeState == ConnectionState.Connected`.

`OverlayPolicy.kt`: as in Rules. `OverlayPrefs.kt`: `load() = settings.settings.first().let { OverlayPrefsState(it.overlayMinimized, if (it.overlayX != null && it.overlayY != null) OverlayPosition(it.overlayX, it.overlayY) else null) }` (use local vals for smart cast); setters delegate to `setOverlayMinimized` / `setOverlayPosition(p.x, p.y)`.

`OverlayContent.kt` (no unit test; checked by screenshot):
```kotlin
private val PanelBackground = Color(0xCC000000)
private fun zoneColor(z: PowerZone?): Color = when (z?.number) {
    1 -> Color(0xFF9E9E9E); 2 -> Color(0xFF2196F3); 3 -> Color(0xFF4CAF50); 4 -> Color(0xFFFFC107)
    5 -> Color(0xFFFF9800); 6 -> Color(0xFFF44336); 7 -> Color(0xFF9C27B0); else -> Color(0xFF616161)
}
private fun statusColor(s: TargetStatus?): Color = when (s) {
    TargetStatus.BELOW -> Color(0xFF42A5F5); TargetStatus.IN -> Color(0xFF66BB6A)
    TargetStatus.ABOVE -> Color(0xFFEF5350); null -> Color.White
}

@Composable
fun OverlayContent(model: OverlayUiModel, minimized: Boolean, onToggle: () -> Unit) {
    if (minimized) Pill(model, onToggle) else Panel(model, onToggle)
}
```
`Panel`: a `Column(Modifier.width(240.dp).background(PanelBackground, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)).clickable(onClick = onToggle).padding(12.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp))` containing, in order: (1) optional banner `Text(model.banner, color = Color(0xFFEF5350), fontWeight = Bold)` when non-null; (2) a `Row` with the zone chip (`Box` with `background(zoneColor(model.zone), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))`, text `model.zoneChip`, 28 sp bold) and the watts (`model.wattsText`, 48 sp bold white) plus a small ` W` label; (3) when `targetText != null`: `Text("Target ${targetText}")` and, when `statusText != null`, a second `Text(statusText, color = statusColor(model.targetStatus), bold)`; (4) interval block: `intervalName` (20 sp), `intervalTime` (36 sp, when non-null), `nextText` (14 sp, 70% white, when non-null); (5) a `Row` of three small stats: `HR hrText`, `RPM cadenceText`, `RES resistanceText`; (6) when `needsFtp`: `Text("Set FTP in PedalMate", color = Color(0xFFFFC107), fontSize = 12.sp)`; (7) the progress bar: `Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(6.dp).background(Color(0x33FFFFFF))) { Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(model.progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)).fillMaxHeight().background(Color.White)) }`. All `Text` are `androidx.compose.material3.Text` with explicit `color = Color.White` (there is no `MaterialTheme` in this window).
`Pill`: `Row(Modifier.background(PanelBackground, RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)).clickable(onClick = onToggle).padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically)` with a 14 dp circle in `zoneColor(model.zone)` and `Text(model.pillText, 20.sp, bold, white)` (red text when `model.banner != null`).

`RideService` changes (replace nothing from A10, only add):
```kotlin
private val uiScope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Main.immediate)
private lateinit var overlay: OverlayController
private lateinit var prefs: OverlayPrefs
private var minimized by mutableStateOf(false)          // androidx.compose.runtime
private var overlayJob: Job? = null

// in onCreate, after the channel:
overlay = OverlayController(applicationContext, getSystemService(WindowManager::class.java))
prefs = OverlayPrefs(container.settings)
overlayJob = uiScope.launch {
    val saved = prefs.load()
    minimized = saved.minimized
    container.session.snapshot
        .map { OverlayPolicy.directive(it.status) }
        .distinctUntilChanged()
        .collect { d ->
            overlay.setKeepScreenOn(d.keepScreenOn)
            if (!d.visible) { overlay.hide(); return@collect }
            val result = overlay.show(
                content = {
                    val model by remember { container.session.snapshot.map { OverlayUiModel.from(it) } }
                        .collectAsState(initial = OverlayUiModel.from(container.session.snapshot.value))
                    OverlayContent(model, minimized) { toggleMinimized() }
                },
                initial = saved.position,
                onMoved = { p -> container.scope.launch { prefs.setPosition(p) } },
            )
            if (result == ShowResult.NO_PERMISSION || result == ShowResult.FAILED) Log.w("PedalMate", "overlay not shown: $result")
        }
}
private fun toggleMinimized() { minimized = !minimized; val m = minimized; container.scope.launch { prefs.setMinimized(m) } }

// onDestroy: overlay.hide(); overlayJob?.cancel(); uiScope.cancel(); scope.cancel(); super.onDestroy()
```
Notes: `setKeepScreenOn` is called before `show` on purpose (it is remembered). `distinctUntilChanged` means a PAUSED to RUNNING change only updates the flag; a `RUNNING` to `FINISHED` change keeps the panel. Because `show()` returns `ALREADY_SHOWN` when visible, repeated directives are harmless. If `show` is refused for lack of permission the ride goes on, and the next status change retries.
In `SetupActivity` delete `OverlayTestRow`, `testOverlay`, `showTestOverlay`, the `show_test_overlay` extra and the file `OverlayTestRow.kt`.

### Task A13: Interval toast

**Goal:** A second, untouchable overlay window shows "NEXT INTERVAL" for 4 s whenever the workout moves to a new interval, centred on screen, never stacking and never intercepting a touch.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/overlay/ToastModel.kt`, `IntervalToast.kt`, `OverlayToastWindow.kt`
- Modify: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ride/RideService.kt`
- Create (tests): `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/overlay/ToastModelTest.kt`, `IntervalToastTest.kt`, `OverlayToastWindowTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: A6 `WorkoutEvent.IntervalChanged`, `StepInfo`; A10 `RideSession.events`; A11 `OverlayWindow`, `ComposeOverlayHost`, `ShowResult`, `FakeWindowManager`; A12 `formatClock`, `formatRange`, `OverlayPolicy`.
- Produces:
```kotlin
// dev.pedalmate.overlay
data class ToastModel(val heading: String, val title: String, val rangeText: String?, val durationText: String) {
    companion object { fun from(e: WorkoutEvent.IntervalChanged): ToastModel }
}
interface ToastWindow { fun show(model: ToastModel); fun hide() }
class IntervalToast(scope: CoroutineScope, window: ToastWindow, dismissAfterMs: Long = 4_000L) { fun show(model: ToastModel); fun cancel() }
class OverlayToastWindow(context: Context, windowManager: WindowManager, canDraw: () -> Boolean = { Settings.canDrawOverlays(context) }) : ToastWindow
```

**Rules:**
- Layout from PedalMate.md, four centred lines: `NEXT INTERVAL` / `ZONE 5` / `253\u2013296 W` style range / `3:00`. `title = to.label.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)` (the preset label `"Zone 5"` becomes `"ZONE 5"`; `"Cooldown"` becomes `"COOLDOWN"`); `rangeText = to.wattRange?.let { formatRange(it) }` (null with no FTP or a zone-less step, and the line is then omitted); `durationText = formatClock(to.seconds * 1000L)`.
- Only `IntervalChanged` shows a toast. `Started`, `Countdown`, `Skipped`, `Finished` never do (the cues cover them).
- Window: `TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY`, flags exactly `FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE or FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE` (touches go straight through to the chess app), `PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT`, `WRAP_CONTENT`, gravity `CENTER`, `x = y = 0`. It reuses `OverlayWindow` and `ComposeOverlayHost`; no drag (pass no-op callbacks).
- No stacking: a new toast replaces the current one (same window, new content) and restarts the 4 s timer; there is never more than one toast window. A burst of toasts leaves only the last one visible. `cancel()` hides immediately and stops the timer.
- Timer: visible for exactly `dismissAfterMs` (still visible at 3999 ms, gone at 4000 ms). All calls happen on the main thread (the service passes its `uiScope`).
- Ride goes idle: the toast is cancelled so it cannot outlive the ride. Overlay permission missing: `show` is a logged no-op.

`IntervalToastTest.kt` (`@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)`; `runTest`; scope is `backgroundScope`; fake window recording `calls: MutableList<String>` as `"show:<title>"` / `"hide"`, plus `var visibleTitle: String?` updated by those calls; helper `fun model(t: String) = ToastModel("NEXT INTERVAL", t, null, "1:00")`):
1. Visible then hidden: `toast.show(model("A"))`; `runCurrent()`; `advanceTimeBy(3_999); runCurrent()`: `visibleTitle == "A"`; `advanceTimeBy(1); runCurrent()`: `visibleTitle == null`, `calls == listOf("show:A", "hide")`.
2. Replacement restarts the timer: `show(A)` at t=0; `advanceTimeBy(2_000)`; `show(B)`; `advanceTimeBy(3_999); runCurrent()`: still `"B"` (6_000 would be the hide time, now at 5_999); `advanceTimeBy(1); runCurrent()`: hidden. A is never hidden separately: `calls == listOf("show:A", "show:B", "hide")`.
3. Burst of three at t=0 (`show(A); show(B); show(C)`): `calls == listOf("show:A", "show:B", "show:C")`, only `"C"` visible; after 4_000 ms one `hide` in total (`calls.count { it == "hide" } == 1`).
4. `cancel()` while visible hides at once (`calls.last() == "hide"`) and the old timer does not hide again later (advance 10_000 s: `hide` count stays 1). `cancel()` when nothing is shown calls `window.hide()` once and does not throw (hiding an already hidden window is the window's concern).
5. A new toast after the previous one fully expired shows again with a fresh 4 s (`show(A)`, `advanceTimeBy(4_000)`, `show(B)`, visible at +3_999, hidden at +4_000).
6. Custom `dismissAfterMs = 1_000` is honoured.

`OverlayToastWindowTest.kt` (Robolectric; `FakeWindowManager`; `allowed` var):
- `show(model)`: one `addView`; params `type == TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY`, `flags == (FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE or FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE)` (exact), `format == TRANSLUCENT`, `gravity == Gravity.CENTER`, `width == height == WRAP_CONTENT`, `x == 0 && y == 0`.
- Second `show(model2)` while visible does NOT add a second window (`added.size == 1`) and does not remove the first (`removed.isEmpty()`).
- `hide()` removes the view and destroys the host (`ViewTreeLifecycleOwner.get(view)!!.lifecycle.currentState == DESTROYED`); `hide()` again is a no-op; `show` after `hide` adds a fresh window (`added.size == 2`).
- `allowed = false`: `show` adds nothing and does not throw; `addFailure = WindowManager.BadTokenException("x")`: `show` does not throw, and the next `show` (failure cleared) works.
- The root view is not touchable by construction: `host.root` has `isClickable == false` and the params carry `FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`ToastModel.kt`:
```kotlin
data class ToastModel(val heading: String, val title: String, val rangeText: String?, val durationText: String) {
    companion object {
        fun from(e: WorkoutEvent.IntervalChanged): ToastModel = ToastModel(
            heading = "NEXT INTERVAL",
            title = e.to.label.uppercase(Locale.ROOT),
            rangeText = e.to.wattRange?.let { formatRange(it) },
            durationText = formatClock(e.to.seconds * 1000L),
        )
    }
}
```
`IntervalToast.kt`:
```kotlin
interface ToastWindow { fun show(model: ToastModel); fun hide() }

/** One toast at a time: a new one replaces the current one and restarts the timer. Main thread only. */
class IntervalToast(
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
    private val window: ToastWindow,
    private val dismissAfterMs: Long = 4_000L,
) {
    private var timer: Job? = null

    fun show(model: ToastModel) {
        timer?.cancel()
        window.show(model)
        timer = scope.launch { delay(dismissAfterMs); window.hide() }
    }

    fun cancel() {
        timer?.cancel(); timer = null
        window.hide()
    }
}
```
`OverlayToastWindow.kt`:
```kotlin
class OverlayToastWindow(
    private val context: Context,
    windowManager: WindowManager,
    canDraw: () -> Boolean = { Settings.canDrawOverlays(context) },
) : ToastWindow {
    private val window = OverlayWindow(windowManager, canDraw)
    private val current = mutableStateOf<ToastModel?>(null)

    override fun show(model: ToastModel) {
        current.value = model
        if (window.isShowing) return                      // content updates through the state
        val host = ComposeOverlayHost(context, { _, _ -> }, {}) { ToastContent(current) }
        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT,
        ).apply { gravity = Gravity.CENTER }
        val result = window.show(host, params)
        if (result != ShowResult.SHOWN) Log.w("PedalMate", "interval toast not shown: $result")
    }

    override fun hide() { window.hide(); current.value = null }
}
```
`ToastContent(state: State<ToastModel?>)` (private composable in the same file): when the value is non-null a `Column(horizontalAlignment = CenterHorizontally, modifier = Modifier.background(Color(0xE6000000), RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)).padding(horizontal = 56.dp, vertical = 32.dp))` with, in order: `heading` 24 sp, 70% white; `title` 72 sp bold white; `rangeText` (only when non-null) 40 sp white; `durationText` 56 sp bold white. All `androidx.compose.material3.Text` with explicit colours. Large text, because the rider reads it from a bike seat.
Service integration in `RideService.onCreate`, next to the overlay code from A12:
```kotlin
private lateinit var toast: IntervalToast
// onCreate:
toast = IntervalToast(uiScope, OverlayToastWindow(applicationContext, getSystemService(WindowManager::class.java)))
uiScope.launch {
    container.session.events.filterIsInstance<WorkoutEvent.IntervalChanged>().collect { toast.show(ToastModel.from(it)) }
}
uiScope.launch {
    container.session.snapshot.map { it.status == RideStatus.IDLE }.distinctUntilChanged().collect { idle -> if (idle) toast.cancel() }
}
// onDestroy, before uiScope.cancel(): toast.cancel()
```
(Subscribe in `onCreate` so the collector exists before any ride starts; `session.events` is a `SharedFlow` with no replay.)

### Task A14: Setup activity (A14H optional split)

**Goal:** The real launcher screen: enter FTP (50..600) with a live zone preview, pick a workout or a free ride, start/pause/skip/stop the ride, launch Lichess, see overlay-permission status, pair a heart-rate strap, and reach the sensor diagnostics. All decisions (FTP validation, selection, start gating, Lichess package fallback) live in plain, unit-tested Kotlin; the Compose screen is thin.

**Split rule (A14H).** This is the largest task. Do Parts 1 to 3 (everything except the heart-rate pairing UI) and commit them. Then do Part H (heart-rate pairing UI) and commit it separately as A14H. If the session is running out, stop after the first commit and report that A14H is left; the main session will dispatch it as its own session. Part H is self-contained below.

**Files (Parts 1 to 3):**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/FtpInput.kt`, `LichessLauncher.kt`, `RideCommands.kt`, `SetupViewModel.kt`, `SetupScreen.kt`
- Modify: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/SetupActivity.kt` (full rewrite)
- Delete: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/debug/RideDebugPanel.kt` (temporary A10 panel). The `debug_ftp` extra hook and the A10 start/stop buttons in `SetupActivity` go away with the rewrite.
- Create (tests): `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/FtpInputTest.kt`, `LichessLauncherTest.kt`, `SetupViewModelTest.kt`; `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/testutil/InMemoryPreferencesDataStore.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: A4 `ZoneTable`, `ZoneRange`; A5 `WorkoutRepository` (`load(): LoadResult(workouts, errors: Map<String, String>)`, workouts sorted by file name), `WorkoutDefinition.totalSeconds`; A8 `SettingsStore(dataStore: DataStore<Preferences>)`, `Settings`, `SettingsStore.DEFAULT_LICHESS_PACKAGE`; A10 `AppContainer`, `RideService`, `RideSession`, `RideSnapshot`, `RideStatus`, `SensorHub`; A12 `formatRange`; A3/A7 `SensorDebugScreen`, `CueDebugRow`.
- Produces:
```kotlin
// dev.pedalmate.ui.setup
sealed interface FtpParse { data class Valid(val ftp: Int); data object Empty; data class Invalid(val reason: String) }
object FtpInput { const val RANGE_ERROR = "FTP must be 50 to 600 W"; const val FORMAT_ERROR = "Enter whole watts"; fun parse(text: String): FtpParse }
object LichessLauncher { const val FALLBACK_PACKAGE = "org.lichess.mobileapp"; fun resolve(configured: String, isInstalled: (String) -> Boolean): String? }
interface RideCommands { fun start(workoutId: String?); fun stop(); fun pause(); fun resume(); fun skip() }
const val FREE_RIDE_ID = "@free"
data class ZoneRow(val zone: Int, val rangeText: String)
data class WorkoutChoice(val id: String, val title: String, val subtitle: String)
data class SetupUiState(
    val loaded: Boolean, val ftpText: String, val ftpError: String?, val zoneRows: List<ZoneRow>,
    val workouts: List<WorkoutChoice>, val loadErrors: List<String>, val selectedId: String,
    val lichessPackage: String, val lichessPackageError: String?,
    val rideStatus: RideStatus, val canStart: Boolean, val startBlockedReason: String?, val startWarning: String?,
    val hrDeviceLabel: String?, val message: String?,
)
sealed interface StartDecision { data class Started(val lichessPackage: String?); data class Blocked(val reason: String) }
class SetupViewModel(settings: SettingsStore, workouts: WorkoutRepository, snapshot: StateFlow<RideSnapshot>, commands: RideCommands, isInstalled: (String) -> Boolean) : ViewModel() {
    val state: StateFlow<SetupUiState>
    fun onFtpTextChanged(text: String); fun select(id: String); fun onLichessPackageChanged(text: String)
    suspend fun start(): StartDecision
    suspend fun onLaunchLichessRequested(): String?     // package to launch, or null (and a message) when Lichess is not installed
    fun stop(); fun pause(); fun resume(); fun skip(); fun dismissMessage()
}
class SetupViewModelFactory(container: AppContainer, commands: RideCommands, isInstalled: (String) -> Boolean) : ViewModelProvider.Factory
```

**Rules:**
- **FTP input (Review Focus 3).** `parse` trims; blank gives `Empty` (meaning "no FTP"); text that is not made only of ASCII digits `0-9` (letters, `.`, `-`, Unicode digits such as `\u0662\u0660\u0660`) gives `Invalid(FORMAT_ERROR)`; more than 4 digits or a value outside 50..600 gives `Invalid(RANGE_ERROR)` (never throws on `"99999999999"`); otherwise `Valid`. A valid value is saved at once (`setFtp`), `Empty` clears it (`setFtp(null)`), `Invalid` is NOT saved (the previous value stays stored) but the text stays in the field with an error under it.
- **Zone preview:** seven rows for a valid FTP (`ZoneRow(n, formatRange(range).removePrefix("Z<n> "))`, so Z1 at FTP 250 reads `0\u2013137 W` and Z7 reads `\u2265 375 W`), none otherwise.
- **Start gating:** `startBlockedReason` is `"Loading settings"` until the first settings emission (`loaded == false`), else `"Fix the FTP value first"` when `ftpError != null`, else `"A ride is already running"` when the status is `RUNNING` or `PAUSED`, else null; `canStart == (reason == null)`. A start without FTP is allowed but `startWarning == "No FTP set: zones and targets will show dashes."` (the ride must not crash without FTP). `FINISHED` does not block a new start.
- `onLaunchLichessRequested()` is the standalone `LAUNCH LICHESS` button: it resolves the package the same way and, when null, publishes the same `Lichess not found` message.
- `start()`: when blocked, returns `Blocked(reason)` and also publishes the reason as `message`; otherwise calls `commands.start(null for FREE_RIDE_ID else the workout id)`, saves `lastWorkoutId`, and returns `Started(pkg)` where `pkg = LichessLauncher.resolve(savedPackage, isInstalled)`; if `pkg == null` it also publishes a `message` containing the words `Lichess not found` and the hint `adb shell pm list packages | grep lichess`. The activity launches Lichess when `Started(pkg != null)`.
- **Lichess package:** default `org.lichess.mobileV2` (the current Flutter app), fallback `org.lichess.mobileapp`; `resolve` returns `configured` when installed, else the fallback when installed (and different from `configured`), else null. Editing the package field saves a trimmed non-blank value (`setLichessPackage`); a blank value shows `"Package name required"` and is not saved.
- **Workout list:** one `WorkoutChoice` per loaded workout (`title = name`, `subtitle = "<totalSeconds / 60> min"` plus `" - <description>"` when a description exists), then a final `WorkoutChoice(FREE_RIDE_ID, "Free ride", "No target, just ride")`. Files that failed to load appear in `loadErrors` as `"<file>: <message>"` while the rest still list. Initial selection: the saved `lastWorkoutId` if it is still in the list, else the first loaded workout, else the free ride. `select(id)` ignores unknown ids and saves `lastWorkoutId`.
- Use `\u2013` / `\u2265` escapes for the en dash and the greater-or-equal sign in source (the tests above spell them that way); no emoji.
- Name clash: `dev.pedalmate.data.Settings` (the stored settings) and `android.provider.Settings` (system settings) share a simple name. `SetupViewModel.kt` imports only the first; `SetupActivity.kt` imports only the second (`canDrawOverlays`, `ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION`) and never needs the first.
- The Activity holds the sensors while visible (`hub.acquire()` in `onStart`, `hub.release()` in `onStop`) so the diagnostics and HR status are live without a ride.

`InMemoryPreferencesDataStore.kt` (test util; lets ViewModel tests stay deterministic and free of real I/O):
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.testutil

import androidx.datastore.core.DataStore
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.emptyPreferences
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow

class InMemoryPreferencesDataStore : DataStore<Preferences> {
    private val state = MutableStateFlow(emptyPreferences())
    override val data: Flow<Preferences> = state
    override suspend fun updateData(transform: suspend (t: Preferences) -> Preferences): Preferences {
        val next = transform(state.value)
        state.value = next
        return next
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the logic**

`FtpInput.kt`:
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.ui.setup

sealed interface FtpParse {
    data class Valid(val ftp: Int) : FtpParse
    data object Empty : FtpParse
    data class Invalid(val reason: String) : FtpParse
}

object FtpInput {
    const val RANGE_ERROR = "FTP must be 50 to 600 W"
    const val FORMAT_ERROR = "Enter whole watts"
    private val digits = Regex("^[0-9]+$")

    fun parse(text: String): FtpParse {
        val t = text.trim()
        if (t.isEmpty()) return FtpParse.Empty
        if (!digits.matches(t)) return FtpParse.Invalid(FORMAT_ERROR)
        if (t.length > 4) return FtpParse.Invalid(RANGE_ERROR)
        val v = t.toInt()
        return if (v in 50..600) FtpParse.Valid(v) else FtpParse.Invalid(RANGE_ERROR)
    }
}
```
(`"0200"` has 4 digits and parses to 200; `"00200"` would be 5 digits and is rejected, which is acceptable.) `LichessLauncher.kt`:
```kotlin
object LichessLauncher {
    const val FALLBACK_PACKAGE = "org.lichess.mobileapp"
    fun resolve(configured: String, isInstalled: (String) -> Boolean): String? = when {
        isInstalled(configured) -> configured
        configured != FALLBACK_PACKAGE && isInstalled(FALLBACK_PACKAGE) -> FALLBACK_PACKAGE
        else -> null
    }
}
```
`RideCommands.kt`: the interface, plus `class ServiceRideCommands(private val context: Context, private val session: RideSession) : RideCommands` (`start` calls `RideService.start(context, id)`; `stop` calls `context.startService(RideService.stopIntent(context))`; `pause/resume/skip` call the session).

`SetupViewModel.kt` (complete):
```kotlin
package dev.pedalmate.ui.setup

const val FREE_RIDE_ID = "@free"

class SetupViewModel(
    private val settings: SettingsStore,
    workouts: WorkoutRepository,
    snapshot: StateFlow<RideSnapshot>,
    private val commands: RideCommands,
    private val isInstalled: (String) -> Boolean,
) : ViewModel() {
    private data class Local(val ftpEdit: String? = null, val selected: String? = null, val pkgEdit: String? = null, val message: String? = null)

    private val local = MutableStateFlow(Local())
    private val loadResult = workouts.load()
    private val choices: List<WorkoutChoice> = loadResult.workouts.map {
        val minutes = it.totalSeconds / 60
        WorkoutChoice(it.id, it.name, if (it.description.isNullOrBlank()) "$minutes min" else "$minutes min - ${it.description}")
    } + WorkoutChoice(FREE_RIDE_ID, "Free ride", "No target, just ride")
    private val loadErrors = loadResult.errors.map { "${it.key}: ${it.value}" }

    val state: StateFlow<SetupUiState> = combine(settings.settings, local, snapshot) { s, l, snap -> build(s, l, snap) }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, build(null, Local(), snapshot.value))

    private fun build(s: Settings?, l: Local, snap: RideSnapshot): SetupUiState {
        val ftpText = l.ftpEdit ?: s?.ftp?.toString().orEmpty()
        val parse = FtpInput.parse(ftpText)
        val ftpError = (parse as? FtpParse.Invalid)?.reason
        val rows = ZoneTable.forFtp((parse as? FtpParse.Valid)?.ftp)?.ranges.orEmpty().map {
            ZoneRow(it.zone.number, formatRange(it).removePrefix("Z${it.zone.number} "))
        }
        val active = snap.status == RideStatus.RUNNING || snap.status == RideStatus.PAUSED
        val blocked = when {
            s == null -> "Loading settings"
            ftpError != null -> "Fix the FTP value first"
            active -> "A ride is already running"
            else -> null
        }
        val pkgText = l.pkgEdit ?: s?.lichessPackage ?: SettingsStore.DEFAULT_LICHESS_PACKAGE
        val hrLabel = s?.let { st -> st.hrAddress?.let { addr -> st.hrName?.let { "$it ($addr)" } ?: addr } }
        return SetupUiState(
            loaded = s != null, ftpText = ftpText, ftpError = ftpError, zoneRows = rows,
            workouts = choices, loadErrors = loadErrors, selectedId = resolveSelected(s, l),
            lichessPackage = pkgText, lichessPackageError = if (l.pkgEdit != null && l.pkgEdit.isBlank()) "Package name required" else null,
            rideStatus = snap.status, canStart = blocked == null, startBlockedReason = blocked,
            startWarning = if (parse is FtpParse.Empty) "No FTP set: zones and targets will show dashes." else null,
            hrDeviceLabel = hrLabel, message = l.message,
        )
    }

    private fun resolveSelected(s: Settings?, l: Local): String {
        val ids = choices.map { it.id }.toSet()
        l.selected?.takeIf { it in ids }?.let { return it }
        s?.lastWorkoutId?.takeIf { it in ids }?.let { return it }
        return loadResult.workouts.firstOrNull()?.id ?: FREE_RIDE_ID
    }

    fun onFtpTextChanged(text: String) {
        local.update { it.copy(ftpEdit = text) }
        when (val p = FtpInput.parse(text)) {
            is FtpParse.Valid -> viewModelScope.launch { settings.setFtp(p.ftp) }
            FtpParse.Empty -> viewModelScope.launch { settings.setFtp(null) }
            is FtpParse.Invalid -> Unit      // never saved: the previous value stays stored
        }
    }

    fun select(id: String) {
        if (choices.none { it.id == id }) return
        local.update { it.copy(selected = id) }
        viewModelScope.launch { settings.setLastWorkoutId(id) }
    }

    fun onLichessPackageChanged(text: String) {
        local.update { it.copy(pkgEdit = text) }
        if (text.isNotBlank()) viewModelScope.launch { settings.setLichessPackage(text.trim()) }
    }

    suspend fun start(): StartDecision {
        val s = state.value
        s.startBlockedReason?.let { reason -> say(reason); return StartDecision.Blocked(reason) }
        val id = s.selectedId
        commands.start(if (id == FREE_RIDE_ID) null else id)
        viewModelScope.launch { settings.setLastWorkoutId(id) }
        val pkg = LichessLauncher.resolve(settings.settings.first().lichessPackage, isInstalled)
        if (pkg == null) say(LICHESS_MISSING)
        return StartDecision.Started(pkg)
    }

    suspend fun onLaunchLichessRequested(): String? {
        val pkg = LichessLauncher.resolve(settings.settings.first().lichessPackage, isInstalled)
        if (pkg == null) say(LICHESS_MISSING)
        return pkg
    }

    fun stop() = commands.stop()
    fun pause() = commands.pause()
    fun resume() = commands.resume()
    fun skip() = commands.skip()
    fun dismissMessage() = say(null)
    private fun say(m: String?) = local.update { it.copy(message = m) }

    private companion object {
        const val LICHESS_MISSING = "Lichess not found: run adb shell pm list packages | grep lichess and enter the package under Advanced."
    }
}
```
(The `state` initialiser references `build`, which uses `choices`/`loadResult`/`loadErrors`: declare those three BEFORE `state`, as above.)

class SetupActivity : ComponentActivity() {

            packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(pkg) != null

#### Part H (A14H): heart-rate pairing UI

**Goal:** Scan for BLE heart-rate straps (service `0x180D`), list them, let the rider pick one, and save it so `HeartRateConnector` (A9) uses it. Commit separately: `feat(setup): heart-rate strap pairing UI`.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/heartrate/HrPairing.kt`, `HrPairingController.kt`; `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/HrPairingPanel.kt`
- Modify: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/data/AppContainer.kt` (add `val hrPairing = HrPairing(hub.hr, hrStore)`, declared after `hub`), `SetupScreen.kt`, `SetupActivity.kt`
- Create (tests): `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/heartrate/HrPairingControllerTest.kt`, `HrPairingTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: A9 `BleScanner` (`isBluetoothAvailable()`, `startScan(onDeviceFound, onScanFailed)`, `stopScan()`), `BleDevice(address, name)`, `HeartRateConnector.useDevice/forget`, `ManagedHeartRateDataSource`, `SavedHrDevice`, `SavedHrDeviceStore`, `requiredBlePermissions()`, `SensorFactory.createBleScanner(context)`; A10 `AppContainer.hub`, `hrStore`.
- Produces:
```kotlin
// dev.pedalmate.heartrate
class HrPairing(hr: ManagedHeartRateDataSource, store: SavedHrDeviceStore) { fun use(device: BleDevice); fun forget() }
sealed interface PairingState {
    data object Idle : PairingState
    data class Scanning(val devices: List<BleDevice>) : PairingState
    data class Done(val devices: List<BleDevice>) : PairingState
    data class Failed(val reason: String) : PairingState
    data class Unavailable(val reason: String) : PairingState
    data class Paired(val device: BleDevice) : PairingState
}
class HrPairingController(scanner: BleScanner, pairing: HrPairing, scope: CoroutineScope, scanWindowMs: Long = 10_000L) {
    val state: StateFlow<PairingState>
    fun startScan(); fun stopScan(); fun select(device: BleDevice); fun forget()
}
```

**Rules:**
- `HrPairing.use(d)`: if `hr is HeartRateConnector`, `hr.useDevice(d)` (saves and reconnects now); otherwise (mock flavor) `store.save(SavedHrDevice(d.address, d.name))`. `forget()`: `hr.forget()` for a connector, else `store.save(null)`.
- `startScan()`: if `!scanner.isBluetoothAvailable()` state becomes `Unavailable("Bluetooth is off or missing")` and the scanner is not touched; if already `Scanning`, ignored; otherwise state `Scanning(emptyList())` and `scanner.startScan(...)`. Devices are de-duplicated by address (a repeated address updates the name if the new one is non-null) and sorted by name (case-insensitive, null names last) then address. Callbacks arrive on binder threads: guard the list with `synchronized`. After `scanWindowMs` the scan stops and the state becomes `Done(devices)`. `onScanFailed(reason)` stops the scan and gives `Failed(reason)`. Callbacks after the scan stopped (late results) are ignored. `stopScan()` stops the scanner, cancels the timer and returns to `Idle`. `select(d)` stops any scan, calls `pairing.use(d)` and gives `Paired(d)`. `forget()` calls `pairing.forget()` and gives `Idle`.
- Permission: the Activity requests `requiredBlePermissions()` with `ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()` before the first scan; on denial show the message `Bluetooth scan needs the Location permission. Run: adb shell pm grant <packageName> android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION` (A15 turns this into a proper card). Read `BlePermissions.kt` for the copied helpers; use only `requiredBlePermissions()`.
- The saved device shows in the setup HR row (`hrDeviceLabel` comes from settings, so it updates by itself after `use`).

- [ ] **Step H1: Write the failing tests**

`HrPairingControllerTest.kt` (`runTest`; the controller scope is `backgroundScope`; `FakeScanner` implementing `BleScanner` records `scanning`, `startCalls`, `stopCalls`, keeps the callbacks, and has `var available = true` plus `fun found(address, name)` and `fun fail(reason)`; `FakePairing` is a `HrPairing` built over a `FakeHr` (from `RideFakes.kt`, A10) and an in-memory `SavedHrDeviceStore` fake whose `saved` var records the last `save`):
1. start: `startScan()`: `state == Scanning(emptyList())`, `startCalls == 1`.
2. results: `found("B", "Zeta")`, `found("A", "alpha")`, `found("C", null)`: `Scanning` list order is alpha (A), Zeta (B), C (null name last); `found("A", "alpha")` again does not duplicate; `found("C", "Strap")` updates C's name and re-sorts it between alpha and Zeta.
3. window end: `advanceTimeBy(9_999)` still `Scanning`; `advanceTimeBy(1); runCurrent()` gives `Done(devices)` with the same list and `stopCalls == 1`.
4. failure: `fail("SCAN_FAILED_FEATURE_UNSUPPORTED")` gives `Failed("SCAN_FAILED_FEATURE_UNSUPPORTED")` and `stopCalls == 1`; after that, advancing 20 s does not change the state.
5. bluetooth unavailable: `available = false`: `Unavailable(...)`, `startCalls == 0`.
6. double start: second `startScan()` while scanning is ignored (`startCalls == 1`).
7. stop: `stopScan()` gives `Idle`, `stopCalls == 1`; a later `found(...)` is ignored; advancing 20 s changes nothing.
8. select: `select(BleDevice("A", "alpha"))`: `pairing` saved `SavedHrDevice("A", "alpha")`, scan stopped, state `Paired(device)`.
9. late result after the window: `found(...)` after `Done` leaves the list unchanged.
10. a new `startScan()` after `Done` begins again with an empty list.
11. `forget()` clears the saved device and gives `Idle`.
`HrPairingTest.kt`: with a plain `ManagedHeartRateDataSource` fake (not a connector): `use(BleDevice("A", "alpha"))` stores `SavedHrDevice("A", "alpha")`; `forget()` stores null. (The connector branch is covered by A9's `HeartRateConnectorTest` cases 14 and 15; do not duplicate it.)
