Add `hrPairing` to `AppContainer`, build `HrPairingController(SensorFactory.createBleScanner(applicationContext), appContainer.hrPairing, lifecycleScope)` in the Activity (cancel scanning in `onStop`), and wire `onPairHr` and the permission launcher; remove the Part 1 placeholder message.

### Task A15: Permission onboarding

**Goal:** Every permission the app needs has an in-app fix path that works on a Peloton Android build that may lack permission screens: a pure planner decides what to show (system screen, runtime request, or the exact adb command), a thin helper reads the real status, and the setup screen shows one card per permission. The service must keep working when any permission is missing.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/permissions/PermissionPlanner.kt`, `PermissionHelper.kt` (both import `android.provider.Settings`, never `dev.pedalmate.data.Settings`); `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/PermissionsCard.kt`
- Modify: `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ui/setup/SetupActivity.kt` (replaces the minimal overlay grant code from A14 and the A14H permission message), `SetupScreen.kt` (swap the overlay row for the card), `app/src/main/java/dev/pedalmate/ride/RideService.kt` (typed `startForeground` fallback)
- Create (tests): `app/src/test/java/dev/pedalmate/permissions/PermissionPlannerTest.kt`, `PermissionHelperTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: A11/A12 overlay behaviour (`ShowResult.NO_PERMISSION` is already logged only); A14 `SetupActivity`, `SetupScreen`, HR scan flow.
- Produces:
```kotlin
// dev.pedalmate.permissions
enum class PermissionKind { OVERLAY, LOCATION, LOCATION_SERVICES }
sealed interface Fix {
    data object None : Fix
    data class OpenScreen(val intentAction: String) : Fix
    data class RequestRuntime(val permission: String) : Fix
    data class ShowAdb(val command: String) : Fix
}
data class PermissionItem(val kind: PermissionKind, val title: String, val granted: Boolean, val fix: Fix)
object PermissionPlanner {
    fun overlay(granted: Boolean, canResolveSettings: Boolean, pkg: String): PermissionItem
    fun location(granted: Boolean, requestedBefore: Boolean, pkg: String): PermissionItem
    fun locationServices(enabled: Boolean, canResolveSettings: Boolean): PermissionItem
    fun hrScanReady(items: List<PermissionItem>): Boolean     // LOCATION and LOCATION_SERVICES both granted
    fun overlayReady(items: List<PermissionItem>): Boolean    // OVERLAY granted
}
class PermissionHelper(context: Context) {
    fun items(): List<PermissionItem>                          // reads the live state
    fun markLocationRequested()                                // remembers that a runtime request was shown
    fun overlayIntent(): Intent; fun locationSettingsIntent(): Intent
}
```

**Rules (exact strings; they are what the rider reads and pastes into a terminal):**
- `overlay(granted = true, ...)` is `PermissionItem(OVERLAY, "Draw over other apps", true, Fix.None)`. Not granted and `canResolveSettings` gives `Fix.OpenScreen(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION)`. Not granted and not resolvable gives `Fix.ShowAdb("adb shell appops set <pkg> SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW allow")`.
- `location(granted = true, ...)` gives `Fix.None`. Not granted and `requestedBefore == false` gives `Fix.RequestRuntime(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)`; not granted and `requestedBefore == true` (the dialog was dismissed, denied, or never appeared on this firmware) gives `Fix.ShowAdb("adb shell pm grant <pkg> android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION")`. Title `"Location (needed for Bluetooth scan)"`.
- `locationServices(enabled = true, ...)` gives `Fix.None`. Disabled and `canResolveSettings` gives `Fix.OpenScreen(Settings.ACTION_LOCATION_SOURCE_SETTINGS)`; disabled and not resolvable gives `Fix.ShowAdb("adb shell settings put secure location_mode 3")`. Title `"Location services (needed for Bluetooth scan)"`.
- `hrScanReady` is true only when both location items are granted; `overlayReady` only when overlay is granted. Neither blocks starting a ride (the ride must run without them); they only gate the overlay display hint and the HR scan button.
- `PermissionHelper.items()` order: overlay, location, location services. It reads `Settings.canDrawOverlays(context)`, `context.checkSelfPermission(ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION) == PERMISSION_GRANTED`, `LocationManager.isLocationEnabled`, `intent.resolveActivity(packageManager) != null` for the two settings intents, and `context.packageName` for `<pkg>` (so the mock flavor prints `dev.pedalmate.mock`). `requestedBefore` lives in `SharedPreferences("permissions")` key `location_requested`.
- Service never crashes on denial: `RideService.enterForeground()` first tries the typed `startForeground`; on `SecurityException` or `IllegalArgumentException` it logs a warning and falls back to the untyped `startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)`. The overlay already returns `NO_PERMISSION` without throwing (A11, A12).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`PermissionPlannerTest.kt` (pure JVM; `pkg = "dev.pedalmate"`): every case above as a separate assertion: overlay granted/resolvable/unresolvable (check the exact command string `adb shell appops set dev.pedalmate SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW allow`); location granted, not requested, requested before (exact `adb shell pm grant dev.pedalmate android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION`); location services enabled, disabled resolvable, disabled unresolvable (exact `adb shell settings put secure location_mode 3`); the package name is interpolated (`"dev.pedalmate.mock"` appears in the mock commands); `hrScanReady` true/false table (both, only location, only services, neither); `overlayReady` true/false; titles are exactly the strings above.

`PermissionHelperTest.kt` (Robolectric, `@Config(sdk = [29])`; `app = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Application>()`; `shadowOf(app)`):
1. All granted: `ShadowSettings.setCanDrawOverlays(true)`, `shadowOf(app).grantPermissions(ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)`, `shadowOf(app.getSystemService(LocationManager::class.java)).setLocationEnabled(true)`: `items().all { it.granted }`, order OVERLAY, LOCATION, LOCATION_SERVICES.
2. Overlay denied with a resolvable settings activity (register one with `shadowOf(app.packageManager).addResolveInfoForIntent(helper.overlayIntent(), ResolveInfo())` after giving the `ResolveInfo` an `activityInfo` with a name and package): fix is `OpenScreen(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION)`; with nothing registered: fix is `ShowAdb(...)` containing `"appops set ${app.packageName} SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW allow"`.
3. Location denied: first `items()` gives `RequestRuntime(ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)`; after `markLocationRequested()` (and still denied) gives `ShowAdb` with `"pm grant ${app.packageName} android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION"`; the flag survives a new `PermissionHelper(app)`.
4. Location services off: `setLocationEnabled(false)` with no resolvable settings activity gives `ShowAdb("adb shell settings put secure location_mode 3")`.
5. `overlayIntent()` has action `ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION` and data `package:<packageName>`; `locationSettingsIntent()` has action `ACTION_LOCATION_SOURCE_SETTINGS`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run to see failure**

Run: `mise exec -- ./gradlew testMockDebugUnitTest --tests 'dev.pedalmate.permissions.*'` -> compile FAIL.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`PermissionPlanner.kt` follows the Rules literally (build each command with string templates over `pkg`; titles as constants). `PermissionHelper.kt`:
```kotlin
class PermissionHelper(private val context: Context) {
    private val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("permissions", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    fun overlayIntent(): Intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION, Uri.parse("package:${context.packageName}"))
    fun locationSettingsIntent(): Intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_LOCATION_SOURCE_SETTINGS)
    fun markLocationRequested() { prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY, true).apply() }
    private fun canResolve(i: Intent) = i.resolveActivity(context.packageManager) != null

    fun items(): List<PermissionItem> {
        val pkg = context.packageName
        val lm = context.getSystemService(LocationManager::class.java)
        return listOf(
            PermissionPlanner.overlay(Settings.canDrawOverlays(context), canResolve(overlayIntent()), pkg),
            PermissionPlanner.location(
                context.checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED,
                prefs.getBoolean(KEY, false), pkg,
            ),
            PermissionPlanner.locationServices(lm?.isLocationEnabled ?: false, canResolve(locationSettingsIntent())),
        )
    }
    private companion object { const val KEY = "location_requested" }
}
```
`PermissionsCard.kt`: for each item a row: title, `OK` in green or `NEEDED` in amber; for `OpenScreen` a `OPEN SETTINGS` button, for `RequestRuntime` an `ALLOW` button, for `ShowAdb` the command in a monospace, selectable `Text` plus the line `Run this from your PC with adb (OpenPelo), then come back.`. `SetupActivity`: `private var permissionItems by mutableStateOf(emptyList<PermissionItem>())`, refreshed in `onResume`; the actions: `OpenScreen` starts `helper.overlayIntent()` or `helper.locationSettingsIntent()` (by `kind`), `RequestRuntime` calls `helper.markLocationRequested()` then launches the runtime request through the existing `RequestMultiplePermissions` launcher (refresh in its callback), and the HR `PAIR HR STRAP` button now checks `PermissionPlanner.hrScanReady(permissionItems)` first and otherwise shows the message `Fix the Location items under Permissions first` instead of scanning. Remove A14's inline overlay-grant code and the A14H adb message. In `RideService.enterForeground()` add the typed-then-untyped fallback from the Rules (build the notification once, then `try { startForeground(id, n, types) } catch (e: SecurityException) { ... } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) { ... }`).

- [ ] **Step 4: Run all tests, then the verify command**

Run: `mise exec -- ./gradlew testMockDebugUnitTest assembleMockDebug assembleRealDebug` -> `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Emulator check (both paths, denial never crashes)**

```bash
mise exec -- ./gradlew installMockDebug
adb shell appops set dev.pedalmate.mock SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW deny
adb shell pm revoke dev.pedalmate.mock android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION
adb shell settings put secure location_mode 0
adb shell am start -n dev.pedalmate.mock/dev.pedalmate.ui.setup.SetupActivity
sleep 3; adb exec-out screencap -p > /tmp/perm1.png            # three NEEDED rows
# Path A (system screen): tap OPEN SETTINGS on the overlay row: the system "Display over other apps" screen opens; allow there, come back: row turns OK
# Path B (runtime then adb): tap ALLOW on the location row, press Deny in the dialog: the row now shows the pm grant command text
adb shell pm grant dev.pedalmate.mock android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION      # run the shown command: row turns OK after returning
adb shell settings put secure location_mode 3                                       # the shown command for location services: row turns OK
# Denial never crashes:
adb shell appops set dev.pedalmate.mock SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW deny
adb logcat -c
adb shell am start-foreground-service -n dev.pedalmate.mock/dev.pedalmate.ride.RideService -a dev.pedalmate.action.START_RIDE --es workout_id pz-43
sleep 15
adb logcat -d | grep -E "FATAL|AndroidRuntime" | head                               # nothing
adb logcat -d -s PedalMate:V | grep -E "overlay not shown|ride status="             # "overlay not shown: NO_PERMISSION" once, ride lines keep coming
adb shell dumpsys activity services dev.pedalmate.mock | grep -i RideService        # still running
adb shell am start-foreground-service -n dev.pedalmate.mock/dev.pedalmate.ride.RideService -a dev.pedalmate.action.STOP_RIDE
```
The unresolvable-settings adb path (`ShowAdb` for overlay and for location services) cannot be reached on a stock emulator; the unit tests carry it. If there is no emulator, say so explicitly.

**Acceptance:** planner and helper tests green (exact adb commands, runtime-then-adb switch, per-flavor package names); on the emulator the overlay and location items each reach their fix path, and a ride started with the overlay permission denied keeps running with only a logged `NO_PERMISSION`.

### Task A16: On-bike verification (USER-RUN, not implementer work)

**Owner:** the user (rider). No subagent has bike access. This is the Phase A exit gate: **hard gate before any Phase B task (B6 onwards) starts.**

**Prerequisites:** A3V passed; A13 and A15 committed; the `real` debug APK built from the final Phase A commit (`mise exec -- ./gradlew installRealDebug`, or `adb install -r app/build/outputs/apk/real/debug/app-real-debug.apk`); ADB paired over wireless debugging (OpenPelo); an iPhone with a BLE heart-rate relay app (BlueHeart, HeartCast or HR Broadcast) and the Apple Watch worn.

**Do:** follow the "Stage 2" checklist in `docs/verification/phase-a.md` item by item on the Bike+ with the Lichess app installed. In addition, tick these Phase A extras and write the answers beside them in the Stage 2 Notes:
- The Permissions card shows OK for all three items after running the printed adb commands (or after granting on-screen where the firmware allows it); record which path was needed.
- Pair the strap with `PAIR HR STRAP`; the HR value appears in the overlay; walk out of range for 30 s and back (or lock the phone): bpm turns to `--` within about 5 s and returns without restarting the app.
- Run `pz-43` for at least the first two intervals; use SKIP once and see the toast (`NEXT INTERVAL` / `ZONE n` / watt range / duration) over the Lichess board without blocking a move.
- Cut sensor access (`adb shell am force-stop com.onepeloton.affernetservice`) mid-ride: the overlay shows `NO SENSOR` and dashes, never the last watts, and recovers.
- Record the Lichess package that actually resolved (`adb shell pm list packages | grep lichess`) and whether the Start button launched it.
- Record whether the right-docked column covers the Lichess clocks or the resign button, and where you dragged it to.

**Record:** tick the boxes, fill in the blanks, set `Result: PASS` or `FAIL` for Stage 2, paste the relevant logcat and the DB check output. Commit the edited file as `docs: Stage 2 on-bike verification results` (`git add docs/verification/phase-a.md`).

**On FAIL:** ask the main session to dispatch an Opus subagent with the pasted logcat and screenshots; typical suspects: overlay flags and window type (A11), foreground-service type or kill under memory pressure (A10), screen timeout bringing up the Peloton UI (`FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON` in A11), HR address rotation (A9), the Lichess package name (A14).

**Gate:** Phase A is accepted when Stage 1 and Stage 2 both read `Result: PASS`. Phase B planning starts only after that.
